. Furthermore, since
\mathcal{X}
is separable under the supremum norm on
\mathbb{R}
, it remains separable under
\|\cdot\|_{\mathrm{W}_{n}}
. This implies
\sup_{\xi\in\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{}}|V_{n,j}(\xi)|
is measurable.
Since
\Delta_{n}=o(1)
, we assume that
\Delta_{n}\in(0,1)
(for finitely many
n
where
\Delta_{n}>1
, one can use an analogous argument with a courser bound to control
\mathfrak{V}_{n}
). Using Assumption
7
and (8.15)-(8.16) of
52
, we can conclude that conditioned on
M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}
and
\tau^{2}_{1},\ldots,\tau^{2}_{n}
, on
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
we have

\displaystyle\max_{\xi\in\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\mathrm{D}_{i}(V_{i}^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},G_{j})-\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{0},G_{j},M_{i})\big)\big(\xi(M_{i})-\xi_{0}(M_{i})\big)\right|

(S87)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{(1+u)\Delta_{n}+\int_{0}^{\Delta_{n}}\sqrt{\log N(\varepsilon,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{},\|\cdot\|_{\mathrm{W}_{n}})}\mathrm{d}\varepsilon\right\}

(S88)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{(1+u)\Delta_{n}+\int_{0}^{\Delta_{n}}(1/\varepsilon)^{h_{1}/2}\log^{h_{2}/2}(1/\varepsilon)\mathrm{d}\varepsilon\right\}

(S89)

\displaystyle\overset{(1)}{\lesssim}\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{(1+u)\Delta_{n}+\int_{-\log\Delta_{n}}^{\infty}t^{h_{2}/2}e^{-t\,(1-h_{1}/2)}\mathrm{d}t\right\}

(S90)

\displaystyle\overset{(2)}{\lesssim}\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{(1+u)\Delta_{n}+|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\cdot\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n}\right\},

(S91)

for all
j\in[N]
, with probability greater than
1-2\exp(-u^{2})
. In the above display, (1) follows by the change of variable
\varepsilon=e^{-t}
and (2) follows using the property that the incomplete gamma function

\Gamma(x):=\int_{x}^{\infty}e^{-t}t^{s-1}\mathrm{d}t
satisfies
\Gamma(x)/(e^{-x}x^{s-1})=O(1)
as
x\rightarrow\infty
.

For
u:=\sqrt{5\log n}+\sqrt{\log N}
, using (
S83
), the forgoing inequality reduces to

\displaystyle\max_{\xi\in\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\mathrm{D}_{i}(V_{i}^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},G_{j})-\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{0},G_{j},M_{i})\big)\big(\xi(M_{i})-\xi_{0}(M_{i})\big)\right|

(S92)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n}.

(S93)

Applying Hoeffding’s inequality along with the union bound on
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, one can conclude from (
S92
), conditioned on
M_{1},\ldots,M_{n},
and
\tau^{2}_{1},\ldots,\tau^{2}_{n}
,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}_{n}\leq K_{\mathsf{tr},1}\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n}

(S94)

with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
. Here the absolute constant
K_{\mathsf{tr},1}>0
(independent of
\tau^{2}_{1},\ldots,\tau^{2}_{n}
and
M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}
) is chosen large enough. Furthermore, since the constant
K_{\mathsf{tr},1}
is independent of
\tau^{2}_{1},\ldots,\tau^{2}_{n}
and
M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}
, using
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}^{c})\leq 2n^{-2}
, we can also conclude that the assertion holds unconditionally with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
. In other words, with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
, we have

\displaystyle U_{1,n}\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n},

(S95)

both for fixed
M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}
satisfying
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
and unconditionally.

Bounding
U_{2,n}
.

By definition

\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})=\int_{\widetilde{A}_{n}}^{\widetilde{B}_{n}}\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})f_{G,K-p}(v^{2})\,\mathrm{d}v^{2}.

We can add and subtract
f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(v^{2})
to obtain

\displaystyle\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})

\displaystyle=\int_{\widetilde{A}_{n}}^{\widetilde{B}_{n}}\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\bigl(f_{G,K-p}(v^{2})-f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(v^{2})\bigr)\,\mathrm{d}v^{2}

(S96)

\displaystyle+\int_{\widetilde{A}_{n}}^{\widetilde{B}_{n}}\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(v^{2})\,\mathrm{d}v^{2}.

(S97)

If (
15
) holds,
\mathrm{D}_{i}(\cdot,M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})
is the score of the conditional likelihood of
v^{2}
and therefore

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(v^{2})\,\mathrm{d}v^{2}=0.

(S98)

Now, one can decompose the term in the foregoing expression as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(v^{2})\,\mathrm{d}v^{2}

(S99)

\displaystyle=\int_{\widetilde{A}_{n}}^{\widetilde{B}_{n}}\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(v^{2})\,\mathrm{d}v^{2}+\int_{[\widetilde{A}_{n},\widetilde{B}_{n}]^{c}}\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(v^{2})\,\mathrm{d}v^{2}.

(S100)

By Lemma
S1
(5), if we restrict to
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, this tail integral is
O(n^{-2})
uniformly over
i=1,\ldots,n
. Plugging the above in (
S96
), we get using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the definition of Hellinger distance

\displaystyle|\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})^{2}\bigl(f_{G,K-p}(v^{2})+f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(v^{2})\bigr)\,\mathrm{d}v^{2}\right)^{1/2}

(S101)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \times\mathcal{H}\!\left(f_{G,K-p},f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}\right)+\frac{1}{n^{2}}.

(S102)

Using the score bound from Lemma
S1
(1) together with the bounded support assumptions on
G
and the finite moments of the scaled
\chi^{2}_{K-p}
distribution, the integral factor can be shown to be uniformly bounded by a constant depending only on
K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}
using Fubini’s theorem. Hence

|\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})|\lesssim\mathcal{H}\!\left(f_{G,K-p},f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}\right)+\frac{1}{n^{2}}.

Consequently, on
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
we have

\displaystyle|U_{2,n}|\lesssim\mathcal{H}\!\left(f_{G,K-p},f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}\right)\Delta_{n}+\frac{\Delta_{n}}{n^{2}}.

(S103)

Combining the bounds for the Taylor remainder,
U_{1,n}
, and
U_{2,n}
, we conclude that with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Sub}_{n,1}\lesssim\Delta_{n}^{2}(\log n)^{2}+\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n}+\mathcal{H}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\Delta_{n}

when (
15
) and (
16
) holds, both for fixed
M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}
satisfying
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
and unconditionally.

J.1.2
Bounding
\mathrm{Sub}_{n,2}

We again use a second-order Taylor expansion, now applied to
\ell(S_{i}^{2};\widehat{\xi},G,M_{i})
around
\xi_{0}(M_{i})
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(S_{i}^{2};\widehat{\xi},G,M_{i})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(S_{i}^{2};\xi_{0},G,M_{i})

(S105)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D(\xi_{0}(M_{i}),S_{i}^{2};G)\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{0}(M_{i})\big)

(S106)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}H(\widetilde{\eta}_{t,i},S_{i}^{2};G)\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{0}(M_{i})\big)^{2}

(S107)

for some
\widetilde{\eta}_{t,i}
between
\xi_{0}(M_{i})
and
\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})
.
As before, the Hessian term is bounded by
(\log n)^{2}\Delta_{n}^{2}
on
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
. Thus it remains to control the linear term. If (
15
) holds, then
D(\eta,s^{2};G)
is proportional to the score of the full conditional density of
S_{i}^{2}
given
M_{i}
and we have

\mathbb{E}\!\left[D(\xi_{0}(M_{i}),S_{i}^{2};G)\mid M_{i}\right]=0.

Proceeding exactly as in the proof of
U_{1,n}
, using
the chaining argument, we can conclude that

\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D(\xi_{0}(M_{i}),S_{i}^{2};G)\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{0}(M_{i})\big)\right|\lesssim\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n},

with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
.
Therefore on
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Sub}_{n,2}\lesssim\Delta_{n}^{2}(\log n)^{2}+\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n}

(S108)

under (
15
) and (
16
), with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
.

When (
15
) and (
16
) holds, noting that (
J.1.1
) and (
S108
) holds on
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
and
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}^{c}_{\mathsf{tr}})=o(n^{-2})
, we can show that there exists a constant
C_{\mathsf{tr}}>0
such that

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi};G,M_{i})\geq-C_{\mathsf{tr}}\mathfrak{R}_{n,1}(\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}),

with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
.
∎

J.2
Hellinger large deviation

The next step to prove the asymptotic FDR control is to prove a large deviation property for

\mathcal{H}^{2}(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}),

where the Hellinger distance between the densities of two mixtures of scaled
\chi^{2}_{K-p}
random variables is defined in (13) of
22
.

Lemma S9
.

Assume that
\tau^{2}_{i}\mid M_{i}\,{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\sim}\limits^{\text{iid}}}\,}G
for all
i\in[n]
(cf. (
16
)), where
G\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}
. Fix an absolute constant
c_{0}>0
. Then there exist constants
\widetilde{D}>0
and
n_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\mathbb{N}
, depending only on
h_{1},h_{2},K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},
and
\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, such that for all
n\geq n_{\mathsf{tr}}
,

\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}\penalty\ \lambda^{2}_{n}\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}+\left(1+\log\log n+\log|\log\Delta_{n}|\right)\penalty\ e^{-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}},

where

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}_{n}=\max\left\{\Delta^{2}_{n}(\log n)^{2},\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\Delta_{n},\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n}\right\}.

(S109)

The proof adapts the empirical Bayes arguments of
7
to the present
\chi^{2}
mixture setting.

Proof.

Define the event
\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}}
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}_{0}(M_{i}))}{f_{G,K-p}(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}_{0}(M_{i}))}\geq e^{-n\,C_{\mathsf{tr}}\mathfrak{R}_{n,1}(\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})}\right\},

(S110)

where for any
G^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, the misspecification cost
\mathfrak{R}_{n,1}(G^{\prime})
is defined in Lemma
S8
. By Lemma
S8
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left(\mathcal{H}^{c}_{\mathsf{tr}}\right)=o(n^{-2}).

(S111)

We choose
n
large enough so that
\lambda_{n}\in(0,1)
. Next define
K=\lceil|\log_{2}(1/\epsilon)|\rceil
. For each
k\in\{1,\ldots,K\}
, let

\mathcal{B}_{k}=\left\{G^{\prime}:\operatorname{supp}(G^{\prime})\subset[\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}],\;\mathcal{H}\!\left(f_{G^{\prime},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\in[\mu_{n,k+1},\mu_{n,k})\right\},

where
\mu_{n,k}=\widetilde{C}\,\lambda_{n}^{\,1-2^{-k+1}}
.
Then

\left\{\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{C}^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{2(1-\epsilon)}\right\}\subseteq\bigcup_{k=1}^{K}\{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}\}.

Combining with (
S111
), we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{C}^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{2(1-\epsilon)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\!\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{C}^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{2(1-\epsilon)},\;\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}}\right]+o(n^{-2})

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\!\left[\left\{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\bigcup_{k=1}^{K}\mathcal{B}_{k}\right\}\cap\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}}\right]+o(n^{-2})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}\right\}\cap\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}}\right)+o(n^{-2}).

Thus, it is enough to bound

\smash{\mathbb{P}(\{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}\}\cap\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}})},

uniformly over
k\in[K]
.
Let
\mathcal{S}_{\mathsf{tr},k}=\{f_{G_{j},K-p}:j=1,\ldots,\mathcal{J}_{k}\}\subseteq\mathcal{B}_{k}
be a minimal proper
n^{-2}
-cover of
\mathcal{B}_{k}
under the metric
\|f_{G_{1},K-p}-f_{G_{2},K-p}\|_{\infty}=\sup_{x\geq 0}|f_{G_{1},K-p}(x)-f_{G_{2},K-p}(x)|
. Since
\mathcal{B}_{k}
is a subset of the class considered in Theorem 9 of
22
, it follows from the proof therein that

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{J}_{k}\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}(\log n)^{2}.

(S112)

Moreover, for every
G^{\prime}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}
, there exists
f_{G_{J},K-p}\in\mathcal{S}_{\mathsf{tr},k}
such that

\displaystyle f_{G^{\prime},K-p}(v^{2})\leq f_{G_{J},K-p}(v^{2})+\eta\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq|\mathcal{J}_{k}|}f_{G_{j},K-p}(v^{2})+\eta\qquad\mbox{for all $v^{2}\geq 0$,}

(S113)

where here and below we take
\eta=n^{-2}
. Define the truncation function

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathsf{tr}}(z)=\eta\,\mathds{1}_{\{|z|\leq\overline{B}\}}+\eta\,\frac{\overline{B}^{2}}{z^{2}}\,\mathds{1}_{\{|z|>\overline{B}\}},

(S114)

where

\displaystyle\overline{B}\asymp\frac{\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}{\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\log n.

(S115)

For every
G^{\prime}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}
we have

\mathcal{H}\!\left(f_{G^{\prime},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\leq\mu_{n,k}.

Also define

\displaystyle\gamma_{n}^{2}=\Delta_{n}^{2}(\log n)^{2}+\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n}.

(S116)

Since

\mathfrak{R}_{n,1}(G^{\prime})=\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mathcal{H}\!\left(f_{G^{\prime},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\Delta_{n},

we obtain for every
G^{\prime}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}
,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{n,1}(G^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\Delta_{n}\lesssim\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n}\lesssim\mu_{n,k+1}^{2},

(S117)

where the penultimate inequality follows from (
S116
).
Indeed,
\gamma_{n}\lesssim\lambda_{n}
by definition of
\lambda_{n}
, and
\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n}\leq\widetilde{C}\,\lambda_{n}^{2-2^{-k+1}}\asymp\mu_{n,k+1}^{2}
, while
\gamma_{n}^{2}\leq\lambda_{n}^{2}\leq\mu_{n,k+1}^{2}
since
\lambda_{n}\in(0,1)
.

Consequently, if
\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}
and
\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}}
holds, then

\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}_{0}(M_{i}))}{f_{G,K-p}(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}_{0}(M_{i}))}\geq e^{-nC_{\mathsf{tr}}\mathfrak{R}_{n,1}(\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})}\geq e^{-nC(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}

for some constant
C>0
depending only on the model parameters.

Let
V_{i}^{2}=S_{i}^{2}/\xi_{0}^{2}(M_{i})
. Using (
S113
) and arguing exactly as in
22
, we obtain, for any
a>1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\left\{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}\right\}\cap\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\!\left[\mathcal{H}\!\left(f_{G,K-p},f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}\right)\in[\mu_{n,k+1},\mu_{n,k}),\;\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}{f_{G,K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}\geq e^{-nC(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\!\left[\sup_{j\in[\mathcal{J}_{k}]}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{G_{j},K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}{f_{G,K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}\geq e^{-nCa(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}\right]+\mathbb{P}\!\left[\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{C_{\sharp}}{\eta_{\mathsf{tr}}(V_{i}^{2})}\geq e^{nC(a-1)(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}\right],

where
C_{\sharp}=C_{\sharp}(K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}})
is the same constant that appears in the proof of Theorem 9 in
22
.

For the first term, since (
15
) holds, the variables
V_{1}^{2},\ldots,V_{n}^{2}
are i.i.d. with density
f_{G,K-p}
. Therefore, retracing the argument in
22
and using (
S112
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left[\sup_{j\in\mathcal{J}_{k}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{G_{j},K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}{f_{G,K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}\geq e^{-nCa(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j\in\mathcal{J}_{k}}e^{\frac{nCa}{2}(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{G_{j},K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}{f_{G,K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}\right)^{1/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j\in\mathcal{J}_{k}}\exp\!\left(-n\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{G_{j},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)+n\sqrt{2\eta\overline{B}}+\frac{nCa}{2}(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})\right).

Since each center
f_{G_{j},K-p}
belongs to the proper cover
\mathcal{S}_{\mathsf{tr},k}\subseteq\mathcal{B}_{k}
, we have

\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{G_{j},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\mu_{n,k+1}^{2}=\widetilde{C}^{2}\lambda_{n}^{2(1-2^{-k})}.

Hence

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left[\sup_{j\in\mathcal{J}_{k}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{G_{j},K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}{f_{G,K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}\geq e^{-nCa(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}\right]

(S118)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\!\left(-n\widetilde{C}^{2}\lambda_{n}^{2(1-2^{-k})}+n\sqrt{2\eta\overline{B}}+\frac{nCa}{2}(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})+\log|\mathcal{J}_{k}|\right)

(S119)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\!\left(-n\widetilde{C}^{2}\lambda_{n}^{2(1-2^{-k})}+n\check{C}\lambda_{n}^{2(1-2^{-k})}\right),

(S120)

where the last inequality follows from (
S112
) and (
S117
), and those inequalities also determine the absolute constant
\check{C}>0
.
Using (
S112
), (
S117
), and the definition of
\lambda_{n}
, we may choose
\widetilde{C}>0
sufficiently large so that

\mathbb{P}\!\left[\sup_{j\in\mathcal{J}_{k}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{G_{j},K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}{f_{G,K-p}(V_{i}^{2})}\geq e^{-nCa(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}\right]\leq\exp\!\left(-nc_{0}\lambda_{n}^{2(1-2^{-k})}\right)

for some constant
c_{0}>0
. Since
\lambda_{n}\in(0,1)
and
2(1-2^{-k})\leq 2
, it follows that
\lambda_{n}^{2(1-2^{-k})}\geq\lambda_{n}^{2}
. Therefore

\exp\!\left(-nc_{0}\lambda_{n}^{2(1-2^{-k})}\right)\leq\exp(-nc_{0}\lambda_{n}^{2})\leq\exp(-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}),

because
\lambda_{n}^{2}\geq(\log n)^{2}/n
by (
S109
).

For the second term, Lemma S5 of
22
yields, after possibly enlarging
\widetilde{C}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left[\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{C_{\sharp}}{\eta_{\mathsf{tr}}(V_{i}^{2})}\geq e^{nC(a-1)(\gamma_{n}^{2}+\mu_{n,k}\gamma_{n})}\right]\leq\exp\!\left(-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}\right).

(S121)

Combining the two bounds, we conclude that for every
k\in[K]
,

\mathbb{P}\left[\left\{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\mathcal{B}_{k}\right\}\cap\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr}}\right]\leq\exp\!\left(-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}\right).

Using the union bound over
k=1,\ldots,K
and using (
S111
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{C}\,\lambda_{n}^{2(1-\epsilon)}\right]\leq\lceil|\log(1/\epsilon)|\rceil e^{-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}.

(S122)

To finalize the proof, we choose

\displaystyle\epsilon=c\,\min\left\{\frac{1}{|\log\Delta_{n}|},\frac{1}{\log n}\right\},

(S123)

for a sufficiently small absolute constant
c>0
(possibly depending on
h_{1}
and
h_{2}
), so that
\epsilon\in(0,1/2)
for all
n
large enough.
Since
\lambda_{n}^{2}\geq(\log n)^{2}/n
by (
S109
), we have

\lambda_{n}^{-2\epsilon}\leq\left(\frac{n}{(\log n)^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}=\exp\!\left(\epsilon\log n-2\epsilon\log\log n\right)\leq e

for all sufficiently large
n
, and hence
\lambda_{n}^{2(1-\epsilon)}\lesssim\lambda_{n}^{2}
for all sufficiently large
n
. Using (
S122
) with this choice of
\epsilon
gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg[\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{C}\,\lambda_{n}^{2(1-\epsilon)}\Bigg]\lesssim\left(1+\log\log n+\log|\log\Delta_{n}|\right)e^{-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}.

(S124)

Since
\lambda_{n}^{2(1-\epsilon)}\lesssim\lambda_{n}^{2}
, there exists a constant
\widetilde{D}>0
such that, for all sufficiently large
n
,

\left\{\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}\,\lambda_{n}^{2}\right\}\subseteq\left\{\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{C}\,\lambda_{n}^{2(1-\epsilon)}\right\},

and hence

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\!\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}\,\lambda_{n}^{2}\right]\lesssim\left(1+\log\log n+\log|\log\Delta_{n}|\right)e^{-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}}+\frac{1}{n^{2}},

(S125)

which completes the proof.
∎

J.3
Proof of Theorem
8

To prove Theorem
8
, recall that
V^{2}_{i}=S^{2}_{i}/\xi^{2}_{0}(M_{i})
for
i\in[n]
. For any
G^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}
and
\xi\in\mathcal{X}
, define the function

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(z,S^{2}_{i},M_{i};G^{\prime},\xi):=\mathbb{E}_{G^{\prime}}\left[2\Phi(-z/\{\nu\xi(M_{i})\tau\})\mid S^{2}_{i},M_{i}\right],\qquad\mbox{for $z\in\mathbb{R}$}

(S126)

where
S^{2}_{i},M_{i}
are assumed to satisfy distributional specification in (
8
) with
\tau^{2}_{i}\mid M_{i}\,{\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt\sim}\limits^{\text{iid}}}\,}G^{\prime}
.
Next, following the proof of Proposition 11 of
(
22
, Proposition 11)
, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(z,S^{2}_{i},M_{i};G^{\prime},\xi)

\displaystyle:=\frac{C_{K,p}}{f_{G^{\prime},K-p}\!\left(S^{2}_{i}/\xi^{2}(M_{i})\right)}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{(t^{2})^{-\,\frac{K-p+1}{2}}\left(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}(M_{i})\right)^{\frac{K-p}{2}-1}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)\left(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}(M_{i})\right)}}

(S127)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times f_{G^{\prime},K-p+1}(t^{2})\,\mathbbm{1}\!\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)\left(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}(M_{i})\right)+z^{2}/(\nu^{2}\xi^{2}(M_{i}))}{K-p+1}\right\}\,\mathrm{d}t^{2},

(S128)

where
C_{K,p}
is the positive normalising constant from
(
22
, Proposition 11)
with
\nu:=K-p
and

f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p+1}
denoting the mixture density.
Furthermore, for all
i\in[n]
, we can show that

\displaystyle P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{P}^{\text{reg}}(Z_{i},S^{2}_{i},M_{i};G)=\mathrm{P}(Z_{i},S^{2}_{i},M_{i};G,\xi_{0}),\quad\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}=\mathrm{P}(Z_{i},S^{2}_{i},M_{i};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},\widehat{\xi})

(S129)

For any
z\in\mathbb{R}
, let us also define the following:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))

\displaystyle:=f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S^{2}_{i}/\widehat{\xi}^{2}(M_{i})),

(S130)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))

\displaystyle:=f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S^{2}_{i}/\xi^{2}_{0}(M_{i})),

(S131)

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))

\displaystyle:=f_{G,K-p}\!\left(S^{2}_{i}/\xi^{2}_{0}(M_{i})\right),

(S132)

\displaystyle N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{P}(z,S^{2}_{i},M_{i};G,\xi_{0})\,\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i})),

(S133)

\displaystyle N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{P}(z,S^{2}_{i},M_{i};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},\widehat{\xi})\,\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})).

(S134)

Also, introduce the quantity

\displaystyle N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{P}(z,S^{2}_{i},M_{i};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},\xi_{0})\,\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i})).

(S135)

Let us define the set

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\{S^{2}_{i}\in[A_{n},B_{n}]\,\mbox{and}\,M_{i}\in[-\mathrm{W}_{n},\mathrm{W}_{n}]\,\penalty\ \text{for all $i\in[n]$},

(S136)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \|\widehat{\xi}-\xi_{0}\|_{\infty,\mathrm{W}_{n}}\leq\Delta_{n},\penalty\ \mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p}\right)\leq\widetilde{D}\penalty\ \lambda^{2}_{n}\Bigg\},

(S137)

where
A_{n}=\underline{\kappa}n^{-6/(K-p)}
and
B_{n}=(3\overline{\kappa}\vee\widetilde{\kappa})\log n
.
Observe that by Assumptions
6
-
7
, Lemma
S1
, and Lemma
S9
, we can conclude that
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{B}^{c}_{\mathsf{tr}})\lesssim n^{-2}
.

Now observe that that using (
18
), we have

\displaystyle\min\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i},\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\}\geq 2\left(1-\Phi(|z|/\nu\underline{M}^{1/2}\underline{L}^{1/2}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right),

(S138)

for all
i\in[n]
. Then for
|z|\leq\underline{z}:=\nu\underline{M}^{1/2}\underline{L}^{1/2}_{\mathsf{tr}}z_{1-\zeta/2}
, it holds that:

\min\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i},\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\}\geq 2\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{}(z_{1-\zeta/2})=\zeta,

for all
i\in[n]
.
Therefore, we can conclude that
P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta=\zeta-\zeta=0
for
|Z_{i}|\leq\underline{z}
. Furthermore, we also have

\displaystyle\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\right|.

(S139)

Furthermore, observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}^{c}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\frac{1}{n^{2}}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S140)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{2}}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right].

(S141)

Now, define
\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i}):=(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))+\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i})))/2,
for all
i\in[n]
. Proceeding as in the proof of Lemma S9 of
22
, we can show using
P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i},\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\in[0,1]
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\cdot\mathds{1}(|Z_{i}|\geq\underline{z})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(Z_{i},S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(Z_{i},S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\cdot\mathds{1}(|Z_{i}|\geq\underline{z})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}-\frac{N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})}{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right].

(S142)

Let us define

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S143)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right].

(S144)

Controlling
\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}
.

We shall show that when we restrict ourselves to
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}
and
|z|\geq\underline{z}
,

\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|\leq\zeta_{n},

almost surely, for some sequence
\zeta_{n}>0
to be specified later. Then, proceeding as in the proof of Lemma S9 of
22
, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}\lesssim\zeta_{n}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1,\star}(S^{2}_{1},M_{1})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\zeta_{n}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\mathds{1}\{S^{2}_{1}\leq B_{n}\}}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1,\star}(S^{2}_{1},M_{1})}\right]

(S145)

\displaystyle=\zeta_{n}\;\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\mathds{1}\{S^{2}_{1}\leq B_{n}\}}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1,\star}(S^{2}_{1},M_{1})}\Bigg\lvert\,M_{1}\right]\right]

(S146)

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot\zeta_{n}\;\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\mathds{1}\{S^{2}_{1}\leq B_{n}\}}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}(S_{1}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{1}))}\Bigg\lvert\,M_{1}\right]\right]\overset{(1)}{\lesssim}B_{n}\zeta_{n}=\zeta_{n}(\log n),

(S147)

where inequality (1) follows since the density of
S^{2}_{1}\mid M_{1}
is proportional to
\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}(S_{1}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{1}))
with the Jacobian term uniformly bounded in
[\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}}]
. Furthermore, in the derivation, we have used the fact that the pairs
(S^{2}_{1},M_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},M_{n})
are identically distributed.
Using the triangle inequality, we can show that for any
|z|\geq\underline{z}

\displaystyle\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|

(S148)

\displaystyle\leq\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|+\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|.

(S149)

For the first term, we can use the techniques in Lemma S9 of
22
to show that, if we restrict to
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|

(S150)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}},\zeta}\mathcal{H}(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p})|\log\mathcal{H}(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p})|^{1/2}

(S151)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{n}\sqrt{|\log\lambda_{n}|},\qquad\mbox{where $\lambda_{n}$ is defined in \eqref{eq:def_lambda_trd_n}}.

(S152)

From the definition of
\lambda_{n}
and using
\Delta_{n}\ll 1
, we can conclude that
|\log\lambda_{n}|\lesssim(\log n).
Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},G)-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|

(S153)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{n}\sqrt{|\log\lambda_{n}|}\lesssim\max\left\{\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{3/2},\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\},

for all
i\in[n]
.

To control the second term, we consider the function

\displaystyle\widehat{g}_{\mathsf{tr}}(\eta;z,s^{2})

\displaystyle:=C_{K,p}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{(t^{2})^{-\,\frac{K-p+1}{2}}\left(s^{2}/\eta^{2}\right)^{\frac{K-p}{2}-1}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)\left(s^{2}/\eta^{2}\right)}}

(S154)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p+1}(t^{2})\,\mathbbm{1}\!\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)\left(s^{2}/\eta^{2}\right)+z^{2}/(\nu^{2}\eta^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\,dt^{2},

(S155)

for any
s^{2}>0
and
|z|\geq\underline{z}
. Observe that

\displaystyle N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})

\displaystyle:=\widehat{g}_{\mathsf{tr}}(\xi_{0}(M_{i});z,S^{2}_{i})

(S156)

\displaystyle N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})

\displaystyle:=\widehat{g}_{\mathsf{tr}}(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i});z,S^{2}_{i}).

(S157)

Then using the expression of
\frac{d}{d\eta}\widehat{g}_{\mathsf{tr}}(\eta;z,s^{2})
from Lemma
S3
and Assumption
7
, we can conclude that for all
i\in[n]
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{d}{d\eta}\widehat{g}_{\mathsf{tr}}(\check{\eta}_{i};z,S^{2}_{i})\right|\lesssim_{\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{z}}(\log n),

(S158)

if we restrict ourselves to
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, where

\check{\eta}_{i}=t_{i}\xi_{0}(M_{i})+(1-t_{i})\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}),\qquad\mbox{for any $t_{1},\ldots,t_{n}\in[0,1]$.}

Consequently using Assumption
7
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{0},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}},\zeta}(\log n)\penalty\ \Delta_{n}.

(S159)

Therefore, we choose

\zeta_{n}=\max\left\{\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{3/2},\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\},

and conclude using (
S145
) and (
S159
), we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max\Big\{\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{5/2},\frac{(\log n)^{5/2}}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big\},

(S160)

where the absolute constants in the foregoing inequality depend only on
h_{1},h_{2},K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}
and
\zeta
.

Controlling
\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}
.

To control
\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S161)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S162)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S163)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S164)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S165)

To analyze the second term, define

\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i}):=\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))+\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{2}.

Then we can write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S166)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S167)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\times\left|\frac{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})-\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right].

(S168)

Next, observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\times\left|\frac{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})-\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S169)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))+\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}\right|\times\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S170)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S171)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right].

(S172)

The foregoing inequality along with (
S166
) allows us to re-write (
S161
) as follows:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S173)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S174)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right].

(S175)

Proceeding as in the proof of Lemma S.10 of
22
and using Lemma
S9
, we can show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S176)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}+\lambda_{n}

(S177)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta_{n}(\log n)+\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n}+\frac{(\log n)^{1/2}}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}.

(S178)

Next, using mean value theorem for the function
\eta\mapsto f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(s^{2}/\eta^{2})
and Lemma
S1
(1), we can conclude that for all
i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))\right|

(S179)

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\xi}-\xi_{0}\|_{\infty}\penalty\ \left(\mathbb{E}_{\tau^{2}\sim\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\left[\frac{2-K+p}{\widetilde{\eta}_{i}}+\frac{(K-p)X}{\tau^{2}\widetilde{\eta}_{i}}\,\Big|\,X=S^{2}_{i}/\widetilde{\eta}^{2}_{i}\right]\right)\times f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S^{2}_{i}/\widetilde{\eta}^{2}_{i}),

(S180)

where
\widetilde{\eta}_{i}=t\xi_{0}(M_{i})+(1-t)\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})
for some
t\in(0,1)
. Since,
S^{2}_{i}\lesssim\log n
for all
i\in[n]
, when we restrict ourselves to
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, using Assumption
7
and observing that
f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S^{2}_{i}/\widetilde{\eta}^{2}_{i})
remains bounded by a constant independent of
n
as
\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\Delta_{n}(\log n).

(S181)

Using the foregoing inequality and the techniques from (
S145
), we can show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{0}(M_{i}))}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{2}.

(S182)

Combining (
S176
) and (
S182
), we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{2}+\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n}+\frac{(\log n)^{1/2}}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}.

Combining (
J.3
) along with (
S160
) and (
J.3
), we can conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta\right|\right]

(S184)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}}\max\left\{\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{5/2},\frac{(\log n)^{5/2}}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\}.

(S185)

This implies Theorem
8
.

J.4
Proof of Proposition
10

The proof of the proposition follows by exactly retracing the arguments of Proposition 15 of
22
along with Theorem
8
.

J.5
Proof of Theorem
12

The proof of the theorem proceeds by integrating Proposition
10
along with the fact that the oracle p-values
\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\}
are critically dense at
\alpha
in the sense Assumption
11
. In particular, this theorem can be proved by following the same strategy adopted for proving Theorem
22
for which we provide a detailed description in Supplement
L.6
.

Appendix K
Proof of asymptotic FDR control in Section
3.2

In this section, we shall consistently assume Assumptions
13
and
14
.

K.1
Approximate NPMLE property of
\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}

We begin by proving an approximate NPMLE property of
\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}
(analogous to Lemma
S8
) under the working model (
22
) and (
23
).

Lemma S10
.

Consider the working model specified through (
15
), (
22
) and (
23
). There exist constants
D_{\mathsf{tr}}>0
and
n_{\mathsf{tr},2}\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}
, depending only on
K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, such that the following hold with probability at least
1-o(n^{-2})
,

\displaystyle\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})}{f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})}\geq e^{-n\,D_{\mathsf{tr}}\mathfrak{R}_{n,2}},

(S186)

where

\mathfrak{R}_{n,2}=\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n}+\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{2}.

However, due to the change in the working model, our proof requires some modification.

Proof.

Observe that under the working model defined by (
15
), (
22
) and (
23
), the factor
\eta^{-2}
disappears from the marginal density of
V^{2}
. Consequently, we redefine

\ell(S_{i}^{2};\xi,G^{\prime},M_{i})=\log f_{G^{\prime},K-p}(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}(M_{i})).

Based on this definition, we aim to show that with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(S^{2}_{i};\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})\geq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(S^{2}_{i};\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G_{\mathrm{mis}},M_{i})-D_{\mathsf{tr}}\,\mathfrak{R}_{n,2},

(S187)

where
\mathfrak{R}_{n,2}
is defined in the lemma statement. As in the proof of Lemma
S8
, we adopt the decomposition of

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(S^{2}_{i};\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ell(S^{2}_{i};\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G_{\mathrm{mis}},M_{i}),

into
\mathrm{Sub}_{n,1}
and
\mathrm{Sub}_{n,2}
(defined in (
S74
) and (
S75
)) and show that

\mathrm{Sub}_{n,1}+\mathrm{Sub}_{n,2}\leq D_{\mathsf{tr}}\,\mathfrak{R}_{n,2},

with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
. Then, arguing as in the proof of Lemma
S8
, the lemma follows.

To control the fluctuation of
\mathrm{Sub}_{n,1}
, we can restrict ourselves to

\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{mis},\mathsf{tr}}=\{|M_{i}|\leq\mathrm{W}_{n},V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\in[\widetilde{A}_{n},\widetilde{B}_{n}]\ \text{for all }i\in[n]\},\qquad\mbox{as in \eqref{eq:cala_trd}.}

By Assumptions
13
-
14
, (
15
), (
22
), (
23
) and Lemma
S1
(3), we have
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{mis},\mathsf{tr}})\lesssim n^{-2}
. Next, we use Taylor expansion of
\ell(S^{2}_{i};\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})
around
\ell(S^{2}_{i};\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})
as before. In that direction, we redefine the gradient and the Hessian functions from
(
S78
) as follows:

\displaystyle D(\eta,s^{2};G^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\frac{\partial}{\partial\eta}\log f_{G^{\prime},K-p}(s^{2}/\eta^{2}),\quad\mbox{and}

(S188)

\displaystyle H(\eta,s^{2};G^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\eta^{2}}\log f_{G^{\prime},K-p}(s^{2}/\eta^{2}).

(S189)

Then, we control

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D(\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}),S_{i}^{2};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})\big)+\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}H(\eta_{t,i},S_{i}^{2};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})\big)^{2}.

(S190)

Within
\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{mis},\mathsf{tr}}
, the Hessian term can be handled using exactly the same technique as in the proof of Lemma
S8
. In particular, using Assumption
14
and Lemma
S1
(2)

\left\lvert\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}H(\eta_{t,i},S_{i}^{2};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})\big)^{2}\right\rvert\lesssim(\log n)^{2}\,\|\widehat{\xi}-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}\|^{2}_{\infty}\lesssim(\log n)^{2}\Delta_{n}^{2}.

For the gradient term, we need to redefine the centering term used to construct
U_{1,n}
and
U_{2,n}
. In particular, we redefine

\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G^{\prime},M_{i}):=\int_{\widetilde{A}_{n}}^{\widetilde{B}_{n}}\mathrm{D}_{i}(v^{2},M_{i};\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G^{\prime})f_{M_{i},K-p}(v^{2})\,\mathrm{d}v^{2},

where

f_{M_{i},K-p}(v^{2})=\int_{0}^{\infty}p_{\chi^{2}}(v^{2}\mid K-p,\tau^{2})\,G_{M_{i},\tau^{2}}(\mathrm{d}\tau^{2}\mid M_{i}),

where
G_{m,\tau^{2}}(\,\cdot\,\mid m)
is the conditional distribution of
\tau_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}=\sigma^{2}_{i}/\xi^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})
given
M_{i}=m
(these are same for all
i\in[n]
by exchangeability).
We decompose

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{D}_{i}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},M_{i};\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})\big)=U_{1,n}+U_{2,n},

where

U_{1,n}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\mathrm{D}_{i}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},M_{i};\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})-\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})\big)\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})\big)

and

U_{2,n}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})\big(\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})\big).

Using the same techniques as the proof of Lemma
S8
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle U_{1,n}\lesssim\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n},

(S191)

with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
. However, due to the different centering used in the construction of
U_{2,n}
, the same analysis will not go through for bounding the fluctuation of
U_{2,n}
. In particular, under (
22
), the score identity (
S98
) does not hold.
Consequently, we cannot get a sharp bound on
U_{2,n}
akin to (
S103
). Instead, we use the definition of
\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})
, along with the definition of
\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{mis},\mathsf{tr}}
to conclude using Lemma
S1
(1) that

|\overline{\mathrm{D}}_{i}(\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},M_{i})|\lesssim(\log n),

where the constant depends on
K,p,\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}}
and
\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}}
. Therefore on
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr},\mathrm{mis}}
, we have

|U_{2,n}|\lesssim\Delta_{n}(\log n).

This implies with probability greater than
1-o(n^{-2})
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Sub}_{n,1}\lesssim\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n}+\Delta_{n}(\log n).

(S192)

Next, we focus on bounding
\mathrm{Sub}_{n,2}
. Once again, we use Taylor expansion to get an identity similar to (
S105
). The Hessian term can be bounded as in the proof of Lemma
S8
. However, under the working model in this framework,
D(\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}),S_{i}^{2};G_{\mathrm{mis}})
is not the conditional score and hence the argument used to bound
U_{2,n}
in Lemma
S8
cannot be used here. Instead, using Lemma
S1
(1), we can show that on
\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, we have

|D(\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}),S_{i}^{2};G_{\mathrm{mis}})|\lesssim(\log n),

where the constant in the inequality depends only on
K,p,\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, and
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{M}_{\mathsf{tr}}
. Therefore, under this framework, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Sub}_{n,2}\lesssim\Delta_{n}^{2}(\log n)^{2}+\Delta_{n}(\log n).

(S193)

Combining (
S192
) and
(
S193
)
, we can conclude the lemma by noting that
\Delta_{n}\ll 1
for large enough
n
.
∎

K.2
Hellinger large deviation

Next, we utilize the approximate NPMLE property of
\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}
to provide a result analogous to Lemma
S9
for
\smash{\mathcal{H}^{2}(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G,K-p})}
under the working model specified by (
15
), (
22
), and (
23
). Under this working model, we have the following result.

Lemma S11
.

Fix an absolute constant
c_{0}>0
. Then there exist constants
\widetilde{D}_{2}>0
and
n_{\mathsf{tr},2}\in\mathbb{N}
, depending only on
h_{1},h_{2},K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\overline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},
and
\overline{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, such that for all
n\geq n_{\mathsf{tr},2}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}_{2}\,\lambda^{2}_{n,2}\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}+e^{-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}},

(S194)

where

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}_{n,2}:=\max\left\{\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{2},\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\Delta_{n},\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/2}\,\Delta^{1-h_{1}/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n}\right\}.

(S195)

Proof.

Define event
\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr},\mathrm{mis}}
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr},\mathrm{mis}}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}{f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}(S_{i}^{2}/\xi^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}\geq e^{-n\,\mathfrak{R}_{n,2}}\right\},

(S196)

where
\mathfrak{R}_{n,2}
defined in Lemma
S10
. By part (2) of Lemma
S10
, any solution
\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}}
of (
19
) satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left(\mathcal{H}^{c}_{\mathsf{tr},\mathrm{mis}}\right)\leq o(n^{-2}),

(S197)

under the working model.
Therefore, for all
\widetilde{D}_{2}>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}_{2}\,\lambda^{2}_{n,2}\right]

(S198)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}_{2}\,\lambda^{2}_{n,2},\,\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr},\mathrm{mis}}\right]+o(n^{-2}).

(S199)

Therefore, we focus on bounding

\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}_{2}\,\lambda^{2}_{n,2},\,\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr},\mathrm{mis}}\right].

We proceed as in the proof of Theorem 9 in
22
by constructing a proper minimal
n^{-2}
cover
\mathcal{S}_{\mathsf{tr},\mathrm{mis}}=\{f_{G_{j},K-p}:j=1,\ldots,\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{mis}}\}\subseteq\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{mis}}
where

\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{mis}}:=\left\{f_{G^{\prime},K-p}:G^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},\,\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{G^{\prime},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}_{2}\,\lambda^{2}_{n,2}\right\}.

Once again, since
\mathcal{B}_{\mathrm{mis}}
is a subset of the class of densities considered Theorem 9 of
22
, therefore

\log\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{mis}}\lesssim(\log n)^{2}.

Recall the truncation function
\eta_{\mathsf{tr}}(\cdot)
introduced in (
S114
).
Proceeding as in the proof of Theorem 9 of
22
, we can conclude that for any
\gamma_{n}>0

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}\right)\geq\widetilde{D}_{2}\,\lambda^{2}_{n,2},\,\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{tr},\mathrm{mis}}\right]

(S200)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\!\left[\sup_{j\in[\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{mis}}]}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{G_{j},K-p}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})}{f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})}\geq e^{-2\gamma_{n}-n\,\mathfrak{R}_{n,2}}\right]+\mathbb{P}\!\left[\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{C_{\sharp}}{\eta_{\mathsf{tr}}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})}\geq e^{2\gamma_{n}}\right].

(S201)

By choosing
\gamma_{n}\asymp(\log n)^{2}
, and noting that the marginal density of
V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}
is
f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left[\sup_{j\in[\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{mis}}]}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{G_{j},K-p}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})}{f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})}\geq e^{-2\gamma_{n}-n\,\mathfrak{R}_{n,2}}\right]\leq\exp(-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}),

(S202)

for some absolute constant
c_{0}>0
, by proceeding as in the proof of Theorem 9 of
22
. Using Lemma S5 of
22
, we can also conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left[\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{C_{\sharp}}{\eta_{\mathsf{tr}}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})}\geq e^{2\gamma_{n}}\right]\leq\exp(-c_{0}(\log n)^{2}).

(S203)

The conclusion of the lemma follows by combining (
S197
), (
S202
) and (
S203
).
∎

K.3
Convergence of estimated p-values

To prove Theorem
15
, we begin by proving the following convergence result of the estimated p-values
\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\}
analogous to Theorem
8
in the misspecified setting.

Proposition S12
.

Under the working model specified by (
15
), (
22
), (
23
), for any
\zeta\in\left(\frac{1}{2},1\right)
, we have a constant
\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{mis}}>0
(possibly depending on
h_{1},h_{2},K,p,\underline{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{M}_{\mathsf{tr}},\underline{L}_{\mathsf{tr}},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}_{\mathsf{tr}}
) such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}_{i}^{\mathsf{tr}}\wedge\zeta\right|\right]\leq\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{mis}}\cdot\mathfrak{L}_{n,\mathrm{mis}},

(S204)

where

\mathfrak{L}_{n,\mathrm{mis}}:=\max\Big\{\Delta^{1/2}_{n}(\log n)^{5/2},\frac{(\log n)^{5/2}}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big\}.

Proof.

To prove the above theorem, recall the Tweedie formula based representation of
\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\}
from (
S127
). One can recognize that
\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\}
by replacing
\xi_{0}
by
\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}
and
G
by
G_{\mathrm{mis}}
in the formula.
In particular, using the definition of the function
\mathrm{P}(\cdot)
,
we can conclude that

\displaystyle P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}:=\mathrm{P}(Z_{i},S^{2}_{i},M_{i};G_{\mathrm{mis}},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}),\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}:=\mathrm{P}(Z_{i},S^{2}_{i},M_{i};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},\widehat{\xi}).

(S205)

One can further rewrite these as a function of only
O_{\mathrm{mis},i}:=Z_{i}/\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}),\widehat{O}_{i}:=Z_{i}/\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}),V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}=S^{2}_{i}/\xi^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})
and
\widehat{V}^{2}_{i}=S^{2}_{i}/\widehat{\xi}^{2}(M_{i})
suppressing the dependence on
M_{i}
. In particular,
P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}:=\mathrm{P}^{\text{reg}}_{\mathrm{mis}}(O_{\mathrm{mis},i},V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2};G_{\mathrm{mis}})
and
P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}:=\mathrm{P}^{\text{reg}}_{\mathrm{mis}}(\widehat{O}_{i},\widehat{V}^{2}_{i};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})
,
where

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}^{\text{reg}}_{\mathrm{mis}}(x,v^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}};G^{\prime}):=\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim G^{\prime}}\left[2\Phi(-x/\{\nu\tau\})\mid V^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}=v^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}\right],\quad\mbox{for $x\geq 0$ and $V^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}\sim\frac{\tau^{2}\chi^{2}_{K-p}}{(K-p)}$.}

(S206)

However, it is important to observe that the formulas for the p-values remain the same in both the frameworks described in Sections
3.1
and Sections
3.2
, only the distributional specification changes.
We modify the definition of the set
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}
as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\{|M_{i}|\leq\mathrm{W}_{n},V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\in[\widetilde{A}_{n},\widetilde{B}_{n}],\,\penalty\ \text{for all $i\in[n]$},\penalty\ \|\widehat{\xi}-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}\|_{\infty}\leq\Delta_{n},

(S207)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}\right)\leq\widetilde{D}_{2}\penalty\ \lambda^{2}_{n,2}\Bigg\},

(S208)

where the quantities
\widetilde{A}_{n},\widetilde{B}_{n},
and
\lambda_{n,2}
are defined in the statement of Lemma
S11
, Assumption
6
, and the proof of Theorem
8
. For any
z\in\mathbb{R}
, let us define the terms corresponding to (
S130
) for this working model. However, to avoid making the paper notation heavy, we shall recycle the notations from (
S130
). In this modified setting, the corresponding quantities are re-defined as follows:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))

\displaystyle:=f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S^{2}_{i}/\widehat{\xi}^{2}(M_{i})),

(S209)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))

\displaystyle:=f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p}(S^{2}_{i}/\xi^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})),

(S210)

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))

\displaystyle:=f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}\!\left(S^{2}_{i}/\xi^{2}_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})\right),

(S211)

\displaystyle N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G_{\mathrm{mis}})

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{P}(z,S^{2}_{i},M_{i};G_{\mathrm{mis}},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}})\,\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i})),

(S212)

\displaystyle N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{P}(z,S^{2}_{i},M_{i};\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},\widehat{\xi})\,\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i})).

(S213)

Using Lemma
S11
, one can show that even under the new working model in this framework

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{\mathrm{mis},i}\wedge\zeta-\widehat{P}_{i}^{\mathsf{tr}}\wedge\zeta\right|\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}+\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}+\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2},

(S214)

where
\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}
and
\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}
are defined analogous to (
S143
) using the terms defined in (
S209
).

First, focus on bounding
\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}
using the techniques developed in the proof of Theorem
8
. However, in this proof we shall replace
\xi_{0}
by
\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}
in all definitions involving the former function. Observe that the arguments in (
S145
) do not replicate in this framework. Nevertheless, under the assumptions of the framework, we have the following

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}\subseteq\cap_{i=1}^{n}\{V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\in[0,\widetilde{B}_{n}]\},

(S215)

where
\widetilde{B}_{n}
is defined in Lemma
S1
. This implies
\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\leq\mathds{1}\{V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\in[0,\widetilde{B}_{n}]\}
. Now, observe that the unconditional density of
V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}
under this framework is exactly
\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}):=f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p}(V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2})
. Since
\widetilde{B}_{n}\asymp\log n
, therefore if

\displaystyle\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G_{\mathrm{mis}})-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|\leq\zeta_{n,\mathrm{mis}},

(S216)

for some sequence
\zeta_{n,\mathrm{mis}}
(independent of
M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}
), then
\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}\lesssim\zeta_{n,\mathrm{mis}}(\log n)
. We shall prove that
\zeta_{n,\mathrm{mis}}
exists.
Recall the decomposition of the left hand side of (
S216
) from (
S148
) as

\displaystyle\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G_{\mathrm{mis}})-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|

(S217)

\displaystyle\leq\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G_{\mathrm{mis}})-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|

(S218)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\widehat{\xi},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|.

(S219)

Observe that bounding the first term in that decomposition is a Cauchy-Schwarz argument that results in

\mathcal{H}(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p})|\log\mathcal{H}(f_{\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}},K-p},f_{G_{\mathrm{mis}},K-p})|^{1/2}.

Under the modified working model, using Lemma
S11

this implies

\displaystyle\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},G_{\mathrm{mis}})-N^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(z,S_{i}^{2},M_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}},\widehat{G}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right|

(S220)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{n,2}\sqrt{|\log\lambda_{n,2}|}

(S221)

\displaystyle\lesssim\max\left\{\Delta^{1/2}_{n}(\log n)^{3/2},\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\},

(S222)

where
\lambda_{n,2}
is defined in (
S195
). Here we have used
\log|\lambda_{n,2}|\lesssim(\log n)
. Next, observe that if we restrict ourselves to
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, the gradient in (
S158
) continues to be uniformly bounded by a constant factor of
\log n
and by Assumption
13
,
\|\widehat{\xi}-\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}\|_{\infty}
is uniformly bounded by
\Delta_{n}
. This implies, even under the working model (
15
), (
22
), and (
23
), the relation in (
S159
) holds with
\xi_{0}
replaced with
\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}
. Consequently

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{1}\lesssim\max\Big\{\Delta^{1/2}_{n}(\log n)^{5/2},\frac{(\log n)^{5/2}}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big\}.

(S223)

For bounding
\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S224)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right].

(S225)

Proceeding as in (
S169
), we get

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))-\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S226)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S227)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right],

(S228)

where
\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})
is defined as

\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i}):=\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))+\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}{2}.

Observing that the marginal density of
V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}
is
\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))
(a function of only
V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}
), one can retrace the arguments from the proof of Theorem
8
to show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S_{i}^{2},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\star}(S^{2}_{i},M_{i})}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]

(S229)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{1/2}_{n}(\log n)+\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n}+\frac{(\log n)^{1/2}}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}.

(S230)

Furthermore, on
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}}
, one can uniformly bound

\left|\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))\right|\lesssim(\log n)\Delta_{n}.

Consequently, we again have

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\widehat{\xi}(M_{i}))}{\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}(S^{2}_{i},\xi_{\mathrm{mis}}(M_{i}))}\right|\mathds{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{tr}})\right]\lesssim\Delta_{n}(\log n)^{2}.

Combining the two foregoing inequalities, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{1/2}_{n}(\log n)^{2}+\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/4}}\Delta^{1/2}_{n}+\frac{(\log n)^{1/2}}{n^{1/4}}|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/4}\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n}+\frac{(\log n)}{\sqrt{n}}.

(S231)

Finally, combining (
S223
) and (
S231
), the lemma follows.
∎

Next, we consider the following lemma on approximate uniformity of
\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}:\theta_{i}=0\}
under (
15
), (
22
), and (
23
).

Lemma S13
.

Fix
\zeta\in(1/2,1)
and assume that
n_{0}/n\rightarrow\pi_{0}\in(0,1)
, as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where
n_{0}=|\mathcal{H}_{0}|
. Then, under the working model specified by (
15
), (
22
), and (
23
), for any
\varepsilon>0

\displaystyle\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathbb{P}[\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}]-t\}\Bigg|\geq\varepsilon\right]=0.

(S232)

Furthermore, we also have

\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\mathbb{E}\left[\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathbb{P}[\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}]-t\}\Bigg|\right]=0.

Proof.

Let us fix
i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}
. Choose
\zeta^{\prime}=(1+\zeta)/2\in(0,1/2)
and
\delta\in(0,\zeta^{\prime})
. Then by Lemma S11 of
22
, we have that for any
t\leq\zeta^{\prime}-\delta

\displaystyle\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}\leq\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t+\delta\}+\frac{1}{\delta}\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|

(S233)

This implies

\displaystyle\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}-t

\displaystyle\leq\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t+\delta\}-(t+\delta)+\delta+\frac{1}{\delta}\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|,

(S234)

which in turn implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}-t\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left\{\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t+\delta\}-(t+\delta)\right\}+\delta+\frac{1}{n_{0}\delta}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|

(S235)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left\{\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t+\delta\}-\mathbb{P}_{G}\left[P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t+\delta\mid V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]\right\}+\delta

(S236)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\frac{1}{n\delta}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|,

(S237)

where we use

\mathbb{P}_{G}\left[P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t+\delta\mid V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]=t+\delta,\quad\mbox{which follows from Lemma\penalty\ \ref{lem:val_p_mis}.}

Taking the conditional expectation, we get

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathbb{P}[\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}]-t\}\right|

(S238)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left\{\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t+\delta\}-\mathbb{P}_{G}\left[P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t+\delta\mid V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]\right\}\Bigg|\,\Big|\,V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]+\delta

(S239)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\frac{1}{n_{0}\delta}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]

(S240)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left\{\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\}-\mathbb{P}_{G}\left[P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\mid V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]\right\}\Bigg|\;\Big|\,V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]+\delta

(S241)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\frac{1}{n_{0}\delta}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right].

(S242)

This also implies

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathbb{P}[\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}]-t\}\Bigg|

(S243)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left\{\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\}-\mathbb{P}_{G}\left[P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\mid V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]\right\}\Bigg|\;\Big|\,V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]+\delta

(S244)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ +\frac{n}{n_{0}}\cdot\frac{1}{n\delta}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right].

(S245)

Recall
\mathfrak{L}_{n,\mathrm{mis}}
from Proposition
S12
. By Lemma
S22
and
n_{0}/n\rightarrow\pi_{0}\in(0,1)
, we get that

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left\{\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\}-\mathbb{P}_{G}\left[P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\mid V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]\right\}\Bigg|\;\Big|\,V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]\leq\frac{\mathfrak{L}^{1/4}_{n,\mathrm{mis}}}{3},

almost surely.
This implies, as
n\rightarrow 0
, we have

\limsup_{n\rightarrow 0}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left\{\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\}-\mathbb{P}_{G}\left[P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\mid V_{i,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]\right\}\Bigg|\;\Big|\,V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]=0,\;\mbox{a.s.}

Next, take
\delta=\mathfrak{L}^{1/4}_{n,\mathrm{mis}}/3
and define
