A_{\mathrm{mis},n}:=\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right]<\frac{\mathfrak{L}^{1/2}_{n,\mathrm{mis}}}{3}\right\}.

Using Proposition
S12
,
n_{0}/n\rightarrow\pi_{0}\in(0,1)
and Markov’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(A^{c}_{\mathrm{mis},n})\lesssim_{\pi_{0}}\mathfrak{L}^{1/2}_{n,\mathrm{mis}}.

(S246)

Since
\mathfrak{L}_{n,\mathrm{mis}}\rightarrow 0
, this further implies

\displaystyle\frac{n}{n_{0}}\cdot\frac{1}{n\delta}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}\right],

(S247)

converges to 0 in probability
(
3
)
.
Therefore, by Slutsky’s theorem for any fixed
\varepsilon>0
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathbb{P}[\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}]-t\}\Bigg|\geq\varepsilon\right]=0.

(S248)

Since
\sup_{t\in[0,1]}|n^{-1}_{0}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathbb{P}[\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}]-t\}|
is bounded, therefore the foregoing relation automatically implies

\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathbb{P}[\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}]-t\}\Bigg|\right]=0,

which further implies using the Jensen’s inequality that

\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\mathbb{E}\left[\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\{\mathbb{P}[\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\mid V_{1,\mathrm{mis}}^{2},\ldots,V_{n,\mathrm{mis}}^{2}]-t\}\Bigg|\right]=0.

This concludes the lemma.
∎

We also have the following (slightly restrictive) non-asymptotic version of the foregoing lemma, which is crucial to prove the FDR control.

Lemma S14
.

Fix
\zeta\in(1/2,1)
and assume that
n_{0}/n\rightarrow\pi_{0}\in(0,1)
, as
n\rightarrow\infty
, where
n_{0}=|\mathcal{H}_{0}|
. Then, under the working model specified by (
15
), (
22
), and (
23
), there exists a constant
\mathfrak{D}_{3,\mathrm{mis}}>0
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,\zeta]}\Bigg|\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}-\pi_{0}t\right\}\Bigg|\right]

(S249)

\displaystyle\leq\mathfrak{D}_{3,\mathrm{mis}}\cdot\max\left\{\Delta^{1/4}_{n}(\log n)^{5/4},\frac{(\log n)^{5/4}}{n^{1/8}}\Delta^{1/4}_{n},\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/8}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/8}\Delta^{\frac{1}{4}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{5/4}}{n^{1/4}}\right\}.

(S250)

Proof.

First, observe that under the working model, using Lemmas
16
and
S22
, we can conclude that for any
t\in[0,1]
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\left(\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\}-t\right)\Bigg|\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{0}}}.

Since
n_{0}/n\rightarrow\pi_{0}\in(0,1)
, therefore we can also conclude that

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\}-\pi_{0}t\Bigg|\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}.

Using Lemma
16
, we can show by retracing the steps in the proof of Proposition 15* of
22
that there exists a
n_{\mathrm{mis}}
such that for all
n\geq n_{\mathrm{mis}}
, any fixed
\zeta\in(1/2,1)
and
\delta\in[0,(1+\zeta)/2]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,\zeta]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}-\pi_{0}t\Bigg|\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}+\delta+\frac{1}{n\delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-\widehat{P}_{i}^{\mathsf{tr}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|\right].

(S251)

By Proposition
S12
, we can take
\delta\asymp\mathfrak{L}^{1/2}_{n,\mathrm{mis}}
(with the constant appropriately chosen to ensure
\delta\in[0,(1+\zeta)/2]
) to get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,\zeta]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}-\pi_{0}t\Bigg|\right]

(S252)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}+\delta+\frac{1}{n\delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left|P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}-\widehat{P}_{i}^{\mathsf{tr}}\wedge\zeta^{\prime}\right|\right]

(S253)

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\Delta^{1/4}_{n}(\log n)^{5/4},\frac{(\log n)^{5/4}}{n^{1/8}}\Delta^{1/4}_{n},\frac{(\log n)}{n^{1/8}}\cdot|\log\Delta_{n}|^{h_{2}/8}\Delta^{\frac{1}{4}(1-h_{1}/2)}_{n},\frac{(\log n)^{5/4}}{n^{1/4}}\right\}.

(S254)

This concludes the lemma.
∎

Next, we consider the proof of Theorem
15
. However, a major part of the proof follows techniques to be described later in the proof of Theorem
22
. In the interest of brevity, we shall not repeat them in this proof and request the readers to refer to the proof of Theorem
22
for the details of the steps.

Proof of Theorem
15
.

Let
\zeta=\max\{3/4,(1+\alpha)/2\}
and recall the Benjamini-Hochberg procedure employing
\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\}
.
For any
t\in(0,1)
, define

R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(t):=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\},\qquad\mbox{and}\qquad V^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(t):=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}.

One can use the self-characterization property of the Benjamini-Hochberg procedure to derive that the BH threshold
\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}
in this framework can be re-expressed as follows:

\displaystyle\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}:=\sup\left\{t\in[0,1]:\frac{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(t)}{n}\geq\frac{t}{\alpha}\right\},

(S255)

The BH procedure rejects all hypotheses
H_{i}:\theta_{i}=0
such that
\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}
. Since the oracle p-values
\{P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\}
are critically dense at
\alpha
, proceeding as in the proof of Theorem
22
, one can show that if we take

\alpha_{0}\in\left(\liminf_{n\to\infty}\inf_{t\in[t_{0},t_{1}]}\frac{1}{nt}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}\!\left[P^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i,\mathrm{mis}}\leq t\right],\alpha\right),

and
\kappa_{n}=5\alpha/4(\alpha-\alpha_{0})
,
then

\displaystyle\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})<n\kappa_{n}\right)=0.

(S256)

Furthermore, by the definition of
false discovery proportion
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{V^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})\vee 1}

\displaystyle=\frac{n\pi_{0}\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})\vee 1}+\frac{n}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})\vee 1}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}\}-\pi_{0}\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}\right\}.

(S257)

Define

\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{mis}}:=\{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})\geq n\kappa_{n}\}.

By (
S256
), we have
\smash{\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F}^{c}_{\mathrm{mis}})=0.}
The definition of
\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}
also implies

\smash{(n/R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}))\leq(\alpha/\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}).}

In particular
\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}\leq\alpha\leq\zeta
.
By the definition of the
false discovery proportion
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{V^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})\vee 1}

\displaystyle\leq\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}^{c}_{\mathrm{mis}})+\frac{V^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})\vee 1}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{mis}})

(S258)

\displaystyle\leq\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}^{c}_{\mathrm{mis}})+\frac{n\pi_{0}\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{mis}})+\frac{n\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{mis}})}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}\}-\pi_{0}\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}\right\}

(S259)

\displaystyle\leq\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}^{c}_{\mathrm{mis}})+\frac{n\pi_{0}\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{mis}})+\frac{n\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{mis}})}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})}\sup_{t\in[0,\alpha]}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}-\pi_{0}t\right\}

(S260)

\displaystyle\leq\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}^{c}_{\mathrm{mis}})+\frac{n\pi_{0}\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}}}{R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{mis}})+\frac{1}{\kappa_{n}}\sup_{t\in[0,\alpha]}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}-\pi_{0}t\right\}

(S261)

\displaystyle\leq\mathds{1}(\mathcal{F}^{c}_{\mathrm{mis}})+\pi_{0}\alpha+\frac{1}{\kappa_{n}}\sup_{t\in[0,\zeta]}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\{\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\}-\pi_{0}t\right|.

(S262)

Taking the expectation on both sides

\displaystyle\operatorname{FDR}^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}\leq\mathbb{P}\left(R^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}(\widehat{t}_{\mathrm{mis}})<n\kappa_{n}\right)+\pi_{0}\alpha+\frac{1}{\kappa_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,\zeta]}\Bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{H}_{0}}\mathds{1}\left(\widehat{P}^{\mathsf{tr}}_{i}\leq t\right)-\pi_{0}t\Bigg|\right].

(S263)

Using Lemma
S14
and (
S256
), we conclude that

\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\operatorname{FDR}^{\mathrm{mis}}_{n}\leq\pi_{0}\alpha.

∎

Appendix L
Proofs of Results from Section
4

In this section, we shall consistently assume Assumption
19
.

L.1
Proof of Theorem
18

Recall the definition

\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,s^{2},a;H):=\mathbb{E}_{H}\left[2\left(1-\Phi\left(\frac{|z|}{\nu\sigma}\right)\right)\bigg|S^{2}=s^{2},A=a\right],

from (
S42
).
Under Assumption
1
, the pair
(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})
satisfies (
13
) and hence the conditional expectation in the definition of the p-value can be expressed as

\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,s^{2},a;H)=\frac{2}{f_{H,K-p}(s^{2},a)}\int_{\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}}\left\{1-\Phi\left(\frac{|z|}{\nu\sigma}\right)\right\}p_{K-p}(s^{2},a\mid\mu,\sigma^{2})\,H(\mathrm{d}\mu,\mathrm{d}\sigma^{2}),

where
p_{K-p}(s^{2},a\mid\mu,\sigma^{2})
is the joint likelihood of
(S^{2},A)
given
(\mu,\sigma^{2})
defined in under (
13
) and
H
is the prior on
(\mu,\sigma^{2})
defined in (
14
).
The marginal density
f_{H,K-p}(s^{2},a)
of
(S^{2},A)
is defined in (
26
).
Define the constant

\mathscr{C}_{K,p}(s^{2}):=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\cdot\frac{(K-p)^{(K-p)/2}(s^{2})^{\frac{K-p}{2}-1}}{2^{(K-p)/2}\,\Gamma\left(\frac{K-p}{2}\right)}.

Without loss of generality, assume
z\geq 0
. Using the integral representation of
1-\Phi(x)
, we write:

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,s^{2},a;H)

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{f_{H,K-p}(s^{2},a)}\int_{\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}}\int_{z}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}\nu\sigma}\exp\left(-\frac{u^{2}}{2\nu^{2}\sigma^{2}}\right)\,p(s^{2},a\mid\mu,\sigma^{2})\,\mathrm{d}u\,H(\mathrm{d}\mu,\mathrm{d}\sigma^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathscr{C}_{K,p}(s^{2})}{f_{H,K-p}(s^{2},a)}\int_{\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}}\int_{z/\nu}^{\infty}(\sigma^{2})^{-(K-p+1)/2}\,\exp\left(-\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+u^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 120.00018pt\times\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sigma}\,\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{K}(a-\mu)}{\sigma}\right)\,\mathrm{d}u\,H(\mathrm{d}\mu,\mathrm{d}\sigma^{2}).

Using (
26
), the above display yields

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,s^{2},a;H)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathscr{C}_{K,p}(s^{2})}{f_{H,K-p}(s^{2},a)}\int_{z/\nu}^{\infty}\Gamma\left(\frac{K-p+1}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{K-p+1}\right)^{(K-p+1)/2}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+u^{2}}{K-p+1}\right)^{-(K-p-1)/2}f_{H,K-p+1}\left(\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+u^{2}}{K-p+1},a\right)\mathrm{d}u.

Finally, by a change of variables
t^{2}=\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+u^{2}}{K-p+1}
, one can match this to the integral representation stated in the theorem, by retracing the steps used to prove Proposition 11 of
22
. This yields

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,s^{2},a;H)=C_{K,p}\cdot\frac{(s^{2})^{\frac{K-p}{2}-1}}{f_{H,K-p}(s^{2},a)}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{(t^{2})^{-\frac{K-p-1}{2}}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)s^{2}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt\times f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},a)\cdot\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\,\mathrm{d}t^{2},

where

C_{K,p}=\frac{\bigl(1+\tfrac{1}{K-p}\bigr)^{-(K-p)/2}\,\Gamma\!\left(\tfrac{K-p+1}{2}\right)}{\sqrt{\pi}\,(K-p+1)^{-1/2}\,\Gamma\!\left(\tfrac{K-p}{2}\right)}.

L.2
Hellinger Convergence of the Estimated Marginal Density

To prove Theorem
20
, we first establish a large deviation inequality for

\smash{\mathcal{H}^{2}\big(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p}\big),}

where for two bivariate densities
g_{1}
and
g_{2}
supported on

\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}\times\mathbb{R}
, corresponding to the joint
distribution of the summary statistics
(S^{2},A)
, the squared Hellinger
distance is defined as

\mathcal{H}^{2}(g_{1},g_{2}):=\int_{\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}\times\mathbb{R}}\left(\sqrt{g_{1}(s^{2},a)}-\sqrt{g_{2}(s^{2},a)}\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s^{2}\,\mathrm{d}a.

In that direction, define the class of joint marginal densities

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}:=\left\{\big(f_{H,K-p},f_{H,K-p+1}\big):\;H\in\mathcal{G}_{H}\right\},

(S264)

where
\mathcal{G}_{H}
is defined in (
25
). To control the metric entropy of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
, fix
\varepsilon>0
and localize the densities to a compact rectangular set

\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon}:=(0,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}]\times[-\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}],

where the truncation levels
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}
and
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}
(depending on
\varepsilon
) are specified below in (
S267
).

For two elements

(f_{H,K-p},f_{H,K-p+1})
and
(f_{H^{\prime},K-p},f_{H^{\prime},K-p+1})
in
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
, define the localized uniform semi-norm

\displaystyle d_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty}\big((f_{H,K-p},f_{H,K-p+1}),(f_{H^{\prime},K-p},f_{H^{\prime},K-p+1})\big)

\displaystyle:=\max\Big\{\|f_{H,K-p}-f_{H^{\prime},K-p}\|_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty},

\displaystyle\hskip 30.00005pt\|f_{H,K-p+1}-f_{H^{\prime},K-p+1}\|_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty}\Big\},

(S265)

where, for
\kappa\in\{K-p,K-p+1\}
,

\|f_{H,\kappa}-f_{H^{\prime},\kappa}\|_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty}:=\sup_{(s^{2},a)\in\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon}}\big|f_{H,\kappa}(s^{2},a)-f_{H^{\prime},\kappa}(s^{2},a)\big|.

We choose
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}
and
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}
so that the
contribution of the tails outside
\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon}

is uniformly negligible over
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
.
Specifically, we require that

\displaystyle\sup_{H\in\mathcal{G}_{H}}\sup_{(s^{2},a)\notin\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon}}\big|f_{H,\kappa}(s^{2},a)\big|\;\leq\;\frac{\varepsilon}{2},\qquad\kappa\in\{K-p,K-p+1\}.

(S266)

To achieve this, define

\displaystyle\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}:=M+\sqrt{\frac{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}{K}\log\!\left(\frac{8}{\pi\varepsilon^{2}}\right)},\quad\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}:=\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}\cdot\max\left\{1,\frac{2}{K-p}\log\!\left(\frac{2}{\varepsilon}\right)\right\}.

(S267)

By Lemma S1C of
22

and the proof of Lemma D.2 in
44
,
this choice ensures that (
S266
)
holds uniformly over
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
.

It remains to bound the metric entropy of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}

under the semi-norm
d_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty}
.
To this end, we construct explicitly an
\varepsilon
-cover
of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
under this semi-norm, which yields
a corresponding upper bound on the log covering number.

Lemma S15
.

Let
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
be the class defined in (
S264
), equipped with the semi-norm
d_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty}
.
Then

\log\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}},d_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty},\varepsilon)\lesssim_{M,\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},K,p}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)\right)^{3}.

Proof.

Fix
\varepsilon>0
. We construct an
\varepsilon
-cover of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
under
d_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty}
. We first approximate the Gaussian component of
f_{H,\kappa}(a,s^{2})
,
for
\kappa\in\{K-p,K-p+1\}
, by Taylor expansion in
a
. Uniformly over
(s^{2},a)\in\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon}
, we get

\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma}\exp\!\left(-\frac{K}{2\sigma^{2}}(a-\mu)^{2}\right)=\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\sum_{i=0}^{J}\frac{K^{i}}{2^{i}\sigma^{2i+1}i!}(-1)^{i}(a-\mu)^{2i}+R_{J}(a,\mu,\sigma^{2}),

with

|R_{J}(a,\mu,\sigma^{2})|\leq\frac{2^{J+1}K^{J+3/2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,\underline{L}^{J+3/2}(J+1)!}\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}^{2J+2},\qquad\mbox{for $a\in[-\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}]$.}

Similarly, expanding the
s^{2}
-dependent component yields

\frac{\kappa^{\kappa/2}}{2^{\kappa/2}\Gamma(\kappa/2)}\exp\!\left(-\frac{\kappa s^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{s^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)^{\frac{\kappa}{2}-1}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}=\sum_{j=0}^{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}}\frac{(-1)^{j}\kappa^{j+\kappa/2}(s^{2})^{j+\frac{\kappa}{2}-1}}{\Gamma(\kappa/2)2^{j+\kappa/2}\sigma^{2j+\kappa}j!}+R_{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}}(s^{2},\sigma^{2}),

where, using Stirling’s bound
(m!)\geq(m/e)^{m}
,

|R_{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}}(s^{2},\sigma^{2})|\lesssim_{\kappa,\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}}(\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon})^{\kappa/2-1}\underline{L}^{-\kappa/2}\left(\frac{e\,\kappa\,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}}{2\underline{L}(\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}+1)}\right)^{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}+1},\quad\mbox{uniformly over $(s^{2},\sigma^{2})\in(0,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}]\times[\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}]$}.

Therefore, if we approximate
f_{H,\kappa}(a,s^{2})
using

\sum_{i=0}^{J}\sum_{j=0}^{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}}\frac{(a-\mu)^{2i}}{(\sigma^{2})^{i+j+\frac{\kappa+1}{2}}},

then the total remainder can be bounded by

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}(s^{2},a)\in\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon}\\
(\sigma^{2},\mu)\in[\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}]\times[-M,M]\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\left\{|p_{\chi^{2}}(s^{2}\mid\kappa,\sigma^{2})||R_{J}(a,\mu,\sigma^{2})|+\left|\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma}\right||R_{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}}(s^{2},\sigma^{2})|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{K}\kappa^{\kappa/2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,2^{\kappa/2}\Gamma(\kappa/2)}(\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon})^{\kappa/2-1}\underline{L}^{-(\kappa+1)/2}\left(\frac{e\,\kappa\,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}}{2\underline{L}(\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}+1)}\right)^{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}+1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\kappa(\kappa-2)^{\kappa/2-1}e^{-(\kappa-2)/2}}{2^{\kappa/2}\Gamma(\kappa/2)}\cdot\frac{2^{J+1}K^{J+3/2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,\underline{L}^{J+5/2}(J+1)!}\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}^{2J+2}.

(S268)

We choose
J
and
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}
large enough to ensure the total remainder is bounded by
\varepsilon/8
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{K}\kappa^{\kappa/2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,2^{\kappa/2}\Gamma\!\left(\frac{\kappa}{2}\right)}(\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon})^{\kappa/2-1}\underline{L}^{-(\kappa+1)/2}\left(\frac{e\kappa\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}}{2\underline{L}(\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}+1)}\right)^{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}+1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\kappa(\kappa-2)^{\kappa/2-1}e^{-(\kappa-2)/2}}{2^{\kappa/2}\Gamma(\kappa/2)}\cdot\frac{2^{J+1}K^{J+3/2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,\underline{L}^{J+5/2}(J+1)!}\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}^{2J+2}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{8}.

(S269)

In that direction, observe that if we choose

J=4\,\max\left\{\frac{2e^{2}K\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\underline{L}},\;\log\!\left(\frac{16}{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\kappa(\kappa-2)^{\kappa/2-1}e^{-(\kappa-2)/2}}{2^{\kappa/2}\Gamma(\kappa/2)}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,\underline{L}^{3/2}}\right)\right\},

then using
(C/m)^{m}\leq e^{-m}
for all
m\geq eC
, we can conclude that the second term in (
L.2
) is bounded above by
\varepsilon/16
.

Similarly, if we choose

\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}=4\max_{\kappa\in\{K-p,K-p+1\}}\left\{\frac{e^{2}\kappa\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon}}{2\underline{L}},\;\log\!\left(\frac{16}{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{K}\,\kappa^{\kappa/2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\,2^{\kappa/2}\Gamma(\kappa/2)}\cdot\frac{(\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\varepsilon})^{\kappa/2-1}}{\underline{L}^{(\kappa+1)/2}}\right)\right\},

then the first term in (
L.2
) is bounded by
\varepsilon/16
.

Thus each density
f_{H,\kappa}
is uniformly approximated on
\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon}

by a polynomial mixture whose coefficients depend only on the moments

\int\frac{(a-\mu)^{2i}}{(\sigma^{2})^{r+\frac{\kappa+1}{2}}}\,H(\mathrm{d}\mu,\mathrm{d}\sigma^{2}),\quad\mbox{where $r=i+j$ and}\;0\leq i\leq J,\;0\leq j\leq\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J},

for
\kappa\in\{K-p,K-p+1\}
. Expanding the polynomial
(a-\mu)^{2i}
for all
i\in\{0,\ldots,J\}
, we get

\sum_{\ell=0}^{2i}\binom{2i}{\ell}(-1)^{\ell}a^{2i-\ell}\int\frac{\mu^{\ell}}{(\sigma^{2})^{r+\frac{\kappa+1}{2}}}\,H(\mathrm{d}\mu,\mathrm{d}\sigma^{2}),\quad\mbox{where $r=i+j$ and}\;0\leq i\leq J,\;0\leq j\leq\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}.

These distinct moment conditions are indexed by
(r,\ell)
with
0\leq r\leq J+\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}
and
0\leq\ell\leq 2\min(r,J)
, giving

\sum_{r=0}^{J+\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}}\bigl(2\min(r,J)+1\bigr)=\sum_{r=0}^{J}(2r+1)+\sum_{r=J+1}^{J+\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}}(2J+1)=(J+1)^{2}+\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}(2J+1)

such linear constraints.
By Carathéodory’s theorem
(see
44
and
22
),
there exists a discrete measure
\widetilde{H}
for the pair
(\mu,\sigma^{2})
supported on at most

\displaystyle\widetilde{J}:=2[(J+1)^{2}+\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}(2J+1)]+1

(S270)

atoms in
[-M,M]\times[\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}]
that matches all these moments.
Consequently,

d_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty}\big((f_{H,K-p},f_{H,K-p+1}),(f_{\widetilde{H},K-p},f_{\widetilde{H},K-p+1})\big)\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{4}.

It therefore suffices to construct a
\varepsilon/4
cover for the class of discrete mixtures
with at most
\widetilde{J}
support points in
[-M,M]\times[\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}]
. To achieve this, it is enough to construct
\varepsilon/12
-covers of
[-M,M]
and
[\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}]
respectively, and an a

\varepsilon/12
-cover of the probability simplex

\Delta(\widetilde{J})
under
\ell_{1}
distance. Let
\mathcal{N}_{\mu}
and
\mathcal{N}_{\sigma^{2}}
be

\varepsilon/12
-covers of
[-M,M]
and
[\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}]
respectively. Since these intervals are compact,

|\mathcal{N}_{\mu}|+|\mathcal{N}_{\sigma^{2}}|\lesssim_{M,\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}.

Let
\mathcal{N}(\Delta(\widetilde{J}))
be a

\varepsilon/12
-cover of the probability simplex

\Delta(\widetilde{J})
under
\ell_{1}
distance.
By Lemma S.1 of
22
,

\log|\mathcal{N}(\Delta(\widetilde{J}))|\lesssim\widetilde{J}\log\!\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right).

One can combine these discretizations to show that
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
admits an
\varepsilon
-cover in terms of
d_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty}
metric whose
cardinality is bounded by

\log|\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}},d_{\mathcal{K}_{\varepsilon},\infty},\varepsilon)|\lesssim\widetilde{J}\log\!\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right).

Using (
S270
),

\widetilde{J}=2[(J+1)^{2}+\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{J}(2J+1)]+1\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L}}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)\right)^{2},

and therefore

\log|\mathcal{N}|\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L}}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)\right)^{3}.

∎

With the above lemma, we can prove the following large deviation inequality for the Hellinger distance between the estimated and the true marginal densities of the pair
(S^{2},A)
. We note that such a result was also shown recently by
21
. For self-containedness, we record our independent proof here as well.

Theorem S16
.

Let
\widehat{H}
be the estimator defined by (
25
). Fix an absolute constant
c_{0}>2
. Then there exists constants
C>0
and
n_{0}\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}
, depending only on
K,p,M,\underline{L},\overline{U}
, such that

\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p}\right)\geq C\frac{(\log n)^{3}}{n}\right]\leq\exp\left(-c_{0}\,(\log n)\right),\quad\text{for all }n\geq n_{0}.

Proof.

Let us define the event

A_{n,\varepsilon_{n}}=\left\{\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p}\right)\geq\varepsilon_{n}\right\},

for some
\varepsilon_{n}
(to be specified later) and aim to characterize
\mathbb{P}[A_{n,\varepsilon_{n}}]
. Consider the subset of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
:

\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\varepsilon_{n}}=\left\{(f_{H^{\prime},K-p},f_{H^{\prime},K-p+1})\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}:\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{H^{\prime},K-p},f_{H,K-p}\right)\geq\varepsilon_{n}\right\}.

Fix
\eta=1/n^{2}
and let
\mathcal{C}_{\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\varepsilon_{n}}}=\{(f_{H_{1},K-p},f_{H_{1},K-p+1}),\ldots,(f_{H_{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}},K-p},f_{H_{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}},K-p+1})\}
be a proper
\eta
-cover of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\varepsilon_{n}}
under the semi-norm
d_{\mathcal{K}_{\eta},\infty}
(defined in (
L.2
)). Let the sub collection of the densities involving degrees of freedom
K-p
be denoted by
\mathcal{C}^{(K-p)}_{\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\varepsilon_{n}}}
. In other words,

\mathcal{C}^{(K-p)}_{\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\varepsilon_{n}}}=\left\{f_{H_{1},K-p},\ldots,f_{H_{\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}},K-p}\right\}.

By the definition of the cover we have for
(f_{K-p},f_{K-p+1})\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\varepsilon_{n}}
,

\inf_{g\in\mathcal{C}^{(K-p)}_{\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\varepsilon_{n}}}}\left\|f_{K-p}-g\right\|_{\mathcal{K}_{\eta},\infty}\leq\eta.

Although Lemma
S15
provides an upper bound on the covering number of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
, the resulting cover may be improper for the subset
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\varepsilon_{n}}
. However, any such improper cover can be converted into a proper one at the expense of doubling the covering radius. Thus,

\log(\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N})\lesssim_{M,\underline{L},\overline{U}}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\right)^{3}.

Let
\mathfrak{R}_{\eta}=(0,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\eta}]\times[-\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\eta},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\eta}]
(where
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\eta}
and
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\eta}
is defined as in (
S267
) with
\varepsilon=\eta
), and define, for
z=(z_{1},z_{2})\in\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}\times\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\eta(z)

\displaystyle=\eta\cdot\mathds{1}\{z\in\mathfrak{R}_{\eta}\}+\frac{\eta A_{\eta}}{z_{2}^{2}}\cdot\mathds{1}\{z_{1}\in(0,B_{\eta}],\,|z_{2}|>A_{\eta}\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta B_{\eta}}{z_{1}^{2}}\cdot\mathds{1}\{z_{1}\notin(0,B_{\eta}],\,|z_{2}|\leq A_{\eta}\}+\frac{\eta A_{\eta}B_{\eta}}{z_{1}^{2}z_{2}^{2}}\cdot\mathds{1}\{z_{1}>B_{\eta},\,|z_{2}|>A_{\eta}\}.

(S271)

Then,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}\times\mathbb{R}}\eta(z)\,\mathrm{d}z\leq 4(A_{\eta}+1)(B_{\eta}+1)\eta.

(S272)

Now, on the event
A_{n,\varepsilon_{n}}
, there exists
\widehat{\imath}\in\{1,\ldots,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}\}
such that

\|f_{\widehat{H},K-p}-f_{H_{\widehat{\imath}},K-p}\|_{\mathfrak{R}_{\eta},\infty}\leq 2\eta.

This implies

f_{\widehat{H},K-p}(s^{2},a)\leq\begin{cases}\max_{j\in[\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}]}\left\{f_{H_{j},K-p}(s^{2},a)+2\eta\right\}&\text{if }(s^{2},a)\in\mathfrak{R}_{\eta},\\
\mathscr{R}_{K,p}&\text{otherwise},\end{cases}

where we define

\mathscr{R}_{K,p}=\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\underline{L}^{3/2}}\cdot\frac{(K-p)^{(K-p)/2}e^{-\frac{K-p-2}{2}}}{2^{(K-p)/2}\,\Gamma\left(\frac{K-p}{2}\right)}.

Define the likelihood ratio

L_{n}(f_{H^{\prime},K-p},f_{H,K-p})=\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{H^{\prime},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}.

Since
f_{\widehat{H},K-p}
is the NPMLE, it satisfies
L_{n}(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p})\geq 1
. Following the proof of Theorem 9 in
22
, we obtain on
A_{n,\varepsilon_{n}}
:

\displaystyle L_{n}(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p})

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\in[\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}]}\left\{\prod_{i:(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})\in\mathfrak{R}_{\eta}}\frac{f_{H_{j},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})+2\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\times\prod_{i:(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})\notin\mathfrak{R}_{\eta}}\frac{\mathscr{R}_{K,p}}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\right\}

\displaystyle=\max_{j\in[\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}]}\Bigg\{\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{H_{j},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})+2\,\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \times\prod_{i:(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})\notin\mathfrak{R}_{\eta}}\frac{\mathscr{R}_{K,p}}{f_{H_{j},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})+2\,\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{j\in[\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}]}\left\{\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{H_{j},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})+2\,\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\times\prod_{i:(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})\notin\mathfrak{R}_{\eta}}\frac{\mathscr{R}_{K,p}}{\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\in[\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}]}\left\{\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{H_{j},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})+2\,\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\right\}\times\prod_{i:(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})\notin\mathfrak{R}_{\eta}}\frac{\mathscr{R}_{K,p}}{\eta(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}.

Fix
\gamma>0
. Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{H}^{2}(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p})\geq\varepsilon_{n}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}[A_{n,\varepsilon_{n}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[A_{n,\varepsilon_{n}}\,L_{n}(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p})\geq 1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{j\in[\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}]}\left\{\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{H_{j},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})+2\,\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\right\}\geq e^{-2\gamma}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\left[\prod_{i:(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})\notin\mathfrak{R}_{\eta}}\frac{\mathscr{R}_{K,p}}{\eta(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\geq e^{2\gamma}\right].

(S273)

Following
22
, using (
S272
) we obtain

\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{j\in[\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}]}\left\{\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{H_{j},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})+2\,\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\right\}\geq e^{-2\gamma}\right]\leq\exp\left(-n\varepsilon_{n}+n\sqrt{8\eta\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{A}_{\eta}\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{B}_{\eta}}+\gamma+\log\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}\right),

for all large
n\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq 0}
.
Define
\varepsilon_{n}=C(\log n)^{3}/n
and
\gamma=nt^{2}\varepsilon_{n}
. Using the independence of
S_{i}^{2}
and
A_{i}
, together with the arguments of
(
22
, Lemma S.5)
and
(
56
, Theorem 1)
, we can choose constants
C,t>0
and
n_{0}\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}
such that for all
n\geq n_{0}
,

\mathbb{P}\left[\prod_{i\in\mathcal{I}^{c}}\frac{\mathscr{R}_{K,p}}{\eta(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\geq e^{2\gamma}\right]\leq\exp\!\left(-c_{0}\log n\right),

for some constant
c_{0}>2
. Furthermore, using Lemma
S15
, we may choose
C>0
sufficiently large so that

\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{j\in[\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{N}]}\left\{\prod_{i=1}^{n}\frac{f_{H_{j},K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})+2\,\eta(S^{2}_{i},A_{i})}{f_{H,K-p}(S_{i}^{2},A_{i})}\right\}\geq e^{-2\gamma}\right]\leq\exp\left(-c_{0}(\log n)\right).

Combining the two preceding bounds completes the proof.

∎

L.3
Proof of Proposition
20

To control the difference between the true
p
-values
\{P^{\mathrm{jt}}_{i}\}
(defined in (
24
)) and the NPMLE-based
p
-values
\{\widehat{P}^{\mathrm{jt}}_{i}\}
(obtained by replacing
H
by the NPMLE
\widehat{H}
), we shall combine the Eddington-Tweedie type representation of
\{P^{\mathrm{jt}}_{i}\}
established in Theorem
18
, along with the control on the estimation error of
f_{H,K-p}
by
f_{\widehat{H},K-p}
in terms of Hellinger distance quantified in Theorem
S16
. However, recall that the Eddington–Tweedie-type formula for the
p
-value involves both the densities
f_{H,K-p}
and
f_{H,K-p+1}
. While the construction of
\widehat{H}
based on the density
f_{\widehat{H},K-p}
ensures that
f_{\widehat{H},K-p}
and
f_{H,K-p}
are close in Hellinger distance, and consequently also in the
L_{2}

distance, this conclusion does not automatically extend to the pair

(f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1},f_{H,K-p+1})
. Here, the
L_{2}
distance between two densities

f
and
g
is defined as

\|f-g\|_{L_{2}}:=\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}\times\mathbb{R}}\bigl(f(s^{2},a)-g(s^{2},a)\bigr)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s^{2}\,\mathrm{d}a\right]^{1/2}.

Nevertheless, under the assumptions of our model, it is possible to transfer the
Hellinger control at level
K-p
to obtain an
L_{2}
bound at level
K-p+1
. In
particular, the following lemma bounds the
L_{2}
distance between

f_{H_{1},K-p+1}
and
f_{H_{2},K-p+1}
, for any priors
H_{1},H_{2}\in\mathcal{G}_{H}
, in terms
of the Hellinger distance between the corresponding densities

f_{H_{1},K-p}
and
f_{H_{2},K-p}
.

Lemma S17
.

Consider the
L_{2}
distance between the densities
f_{H_{1},K-p+1}
and
f_{H_{2},K-p+1}
where the priors
H_{1},H_{2}\in\mathcal{G}_{H}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\left\|f_{H_{1},K-p+1}-f_{H_{2},K-p+1}\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\lesssim_{K,p,M,\underline{L},\overline{U}}\left(1+\left|\log\mathcal{H}\left(f_{H_{1},K-p},f_{H_{2},K-p}\right)\right|\right)\cdot\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{H_{1},K-p},f_{H_{2},K-p}\right).

(S274)

Using the foregoing lemma, it suffices to characterize the convergence rate of

\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p}\right)

which is established in Theorem
S16
, in order to derive a corresponding convergence rate for
f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}
toward
f_{H,K-p+1}
in the
L_{2}
metric defined in (
S17
). The proof of this lemma involves nontrivial arguments based on integral transforms. A similar result was established in Lemma 12 of
22
in the context of univariate densities involving
S^{2}
, where the Mellin transform of the marginal density was employed. However, this approach does not directly apply in our setting, as the joint density of
(S^{2},A)
is not supported on the positive orthant. Instead, we introduce a new integral transform that combines the Mellin transform (for the
S^{2}
coordinate) and the Fourier transform (for the
A
coordinate), and leverage its properties to derive the desired inequality.

Proof of Proposition
20
.

Let us begin by observing that for any joint prior
H^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{H}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H^{\prime})\geq 2\left(1-\Phi(|z|/\nu\underline{L}^{1/2})\right),

(S275)

for
\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(\cdot)
defined in the proof of Lemma
17

Then for
|z|\leq z_{1-\zeta/2}
, i.e.,
|z|\leq\underline{z}:=\nu\underline{L}^{1/2}z_{1-\zeta/2}
, it holds that:

\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H^{\prime})\geq 2\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{}(z_{1-\zeta/2})=\zeta,

where
\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{}(\cdot)
is the survival function of the standard Gaussian distribution. Since both the true mixing measure
H
and the estimated mixing measure
\widehat{H}
belong to
\mathcal{G}_{H}
, therefore we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\geq\zeta,\qquad\mbox{and}\qquad\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})\geq\zeta.

(S276)

Consequently, for all
|z|\leq z_{1-\zeta/2}
, we have

\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})\wedge\zeta-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\wedge\zeta=\zeta-\zeta=0,\quad\mbox{for $|z|\leq\nu\underline{L}^{1/2}z_{1-\zeta/2}$.}

Furthermore, since the projection of a number in
[0,1]
to the set
[0,\zeta]
we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})\wedge\zeta-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\wedge\zeta\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|.

(S277)

Combining the two above relations and letting
\widetilde{Z}_{i}:=\operatorname{sign}(Z_{i})\cdot(|Z_{i}|\vee\underline{z})
, we find the following:

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(Z_{i},S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})\wedge\zeta-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(Z_{i},S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\wedge\zeta\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(\widetilde{Z}_{i},S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(\widetilde{Z}_{i},S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|

(S278)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|.

(S279)

Therefore, it is enough to control

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|\right].

Let
\mathcal{A}
be the event:

\mathcal{A}:=\left\{\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{\widehat{H},K-p},f_{H,K-p}\right)<C\frac{(\log n)^{3}}{n}\right\},\qquad\mbox{for $C>0$ defined in Theorem \ref{thm:double_hellinger}.}

(S280)

Consider the following decomposition.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|\right]

(S281)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{A}^{c}]+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right].

(S282)

In the above decomposition, we have used the fact that
\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H}),\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\in[0,1]
. Next, for any distribution
H^{\prime}
supported on
\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
and any
z\in\mathbb{R}
,
s^{2}>0
,
a\in\mathbb{R}
, let us write:

\displaystyle N(z,s^{2},a;H^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,s^{2},a;H^{\prime})\cdot f_{H^{\prime},K-p}(s^{2},a)

(S283)

\displaystyle N_{i}(z,H^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=N(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H^{\prime}),

(S284)

\displaystyle D_{i}(H^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=f_{H^{\prime},K-p}(S^{2}_{i},A_{i}).

(S285)

By these definitions, it holds that
\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})=N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})/D_{i}(\widehat{H})
. Let us define
\widehat{H}_{\star}:=(\widehat{H}+H)/2
.
Then:

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H})}{D_{i}({H})}-\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H})}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H})}{D_{i}({H})}-\frac{N_{i}(z,{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}+\frac{N_{i}(z,{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}-\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}+\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}-\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N_{i}(z,{H})}{D_{i}({H})}\frac{|D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}({H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}+\frac{|N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}+\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H})}\frac{|D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}+\frac{|D_{i}(\widehat{H})-D_{i}({H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}

In the last step we have used two facts: first, it holds that
N_{i}(z,H^{\prime})/D_{i}(H^{\prime})\in[0,1]
for all
H^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{H}
(since they correspond to conditional
p
-values), and second, the map
H^{\prime}\mapsto D_{i}(H^{\prime})
is linear, which implies that:

D_{i}(H)-D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})=\frac{D_{i}(H)-D_{i}(\widehat{H})}{2},\quad D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H})=\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H})}{2}.

Combining the above results, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|\right]

(S286)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{A}^{c}]+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};\widehat{H})-\mathrm{P}^{\text{jt}}(z,S^{2}_{i},A_{i};H)\right|\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S287)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{A}^{c}]+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\frac{|N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{|D_{i}(\widehat{H})-D_{i}({H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S288)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{A}^{c}]+T_{1}+T_{2},

(S289)

where

\displaystyle T_{1}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\frac{|N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right],\quad\mbox{and}

(S290)

\displaystyle\quad T_{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{|D_{i}(\widehat{H})-D_{i}({H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right].

(S291)

By Theorem
S16
, we have

\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{A}^{c}]\leq\exp\left(-c_{0}(\log n)\right),

where
c_{0}>2
.
Now, we consider the following two lemmas about the terms
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
.

Lemma S18
.

For all
n\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\frac{|N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{13/4}}{\sqrt{n}},

(S292)

where the set
\mathcal{A}
is defined in (
S280
).

Lemma S19
.

For all
n\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}
, we have

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{|D_{i}(\widehat{H})-D_{i}({H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L}}\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}

where the set
\mathcal{A}
is defined in (
S280
).

Plugging in the conclusion of the above two lemmas in (
S286
), the assertion of the proposition follows.
∎

L.3.1
Proof of Lemma
S17

To prove Lemma
S17
, we begin by defining the following integral transform for the density
f_{H,K-p}
in (
26
).

Definition S20

(Mellin–Fourier transform)
.

Let
(X_{1},X_{2})
be a random vector with joint density
f_{H,K-p}(x,y)

corresponding to a prior
H
satisfying

\displaystyle\mathsf{supp}(H)\subseteq[-M,M]\times[\underline{L},\overline{U}].

(S293)

For
c\in\mathbb{R}
, the
Mellin–Fourier transform
of
(X_{1},X_{2})

is defined as

\displaystyle\widehat{f}_{H,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};c)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}x^{\,c-1+it_{1}}\,e^{it_{2}y}\,f_{H,K-p}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}x,

(S294)

whenever the integral is well defined.

Analogously, the same transform can be defined for the density
f_{H,K-p+1}
. Using the arguments from the proof of Lemma 12 in
22
, we show that the integral transform is finite for both
f_{H,K-p}
and
f_{H,K-p+1}
whenever
c+(K-p)/2-1>0
.

With the change of variable
(x,y)\mapsto(e^{u},y)
we may write

\widehat{f}_{H,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};c)=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{it_{1}u}\,e^{it_{2}y}\,F_{H,K-p}(u,y;c)\,du\,dy,

where
F_{H,K-p}(u,y;c)=e^{uc}f_{H,K-p}(e^{u},y)
.
Thus
\widehat{f}_{H,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};c)
is the Fourier transform of the function
F_{H,K-p}(u,y;c)
.
By the Plancherel isometry
(
5
)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\Big|\widehat{f}_{H_{1},K-p+1}(t_{1},t_{2};c)-\widehat{f}_{H_{2},K-p+1}(t_{1},t_{2};c)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{1}\,\mathrm{d}t_{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\Big(F_{H_{1},K-p+1}(u,y;c)-F_{H_{2},K-p+1}(u,y;c)\Big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}u\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{2uc}\,\Big(f_{H_{1},K-p+1}(e^{u},y)-f_{H_{2},K-p+1}(e^{u},y)\Big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}u\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}x^{2c-1}\,\Big(f_{H_{1},K-p+1}(x,y)-f_{H_{2},K-p+1}(x,y)\Big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y,

(S295)

for any pair of densities
H_{1},H_{2}
satisfying (
S293
).
An analogous identity holds for
f_{H_{1},K-p}
and
f_{H_{2},K-p}
. Observe that using the arguments from the proof of
(
22
, Lemma 12)
, we can show that the integral in (
L.3.1
) is finite.

In this proof, we consider the Mellin–Fourier transform of
(S^{2},A)
(denoted by
\mathcal{M}_{c}(t_{1},t_{2};H)
), where

A\sim\mathrm{N}(\mu,\sigma^{2}/K),\qquad(K-p)S^{2}\sim\sigma^{2}\chi^{2}_{K-p},\qquad(\mu,\sigma^{2})\sim H.

We write

\mathcal{M}_{c}(t_{1},t_{2};H):=\mathbb{E}_{H}\!\left[(S^{2})^{c+it_{1}-1}e^{it_{2}A}\right],

Since
S^{2}\sim\sigma^{2}\chi^{2}_{K-p}/(K-p)
and

A\sim\mu+\sigma Z/\sqrt{K}
with
Z\sim\mathrm{N}(0,1)
and the
\chi^{2}_{K-p}
variable independent of
(\mu,\sigma^{2})
. Then, using independence, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};H)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{H}\left[(\sigma^{2})^{c+it_{1}-1}\left(\frac{\chi^{2}_{K-p}}{K-p}\right)^{c+it_{1}-1}e^{it_{2}\left(\mu+\frac{\sigma Z}{\sqrt{K}}\right)}\right]

(S296)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{H}\left[(\sigma^{2})^{c+it_{1}-1}e^{it_{2}\left(\mu+\frac{\sigma Z}{\sqrt{K}}\right)}\right]\,\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\chi^{2}_{K-p}}{K-p}\right)^{c+it_{1}-1}\right],

(S297)

where the expectation is over
\chi^{2}_{K-p}
,
Z
, and
H
.
Note that only the second factor depends on
K
and
p
.

Fix
c
satisfying
c+(K-p)/2-1>0
. By retracing the argument in the proof of Lemma 12 of
22
, we conclude that

\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p+1}(t_{1},t_{2};H)=\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};H)\,\cdot r_{K,p,c}(t_{1}),

for some function
r_{K,p,c}:\mathbb{R}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}
satisfying

\displaystyle|r_{K,p,c}(t_{1})|\leq\mathfrak{K}_{K,p,c}|T_{0}|^{1/2},\quad\text{for all $t\in\mathbb{R}$ with $|t_{1}|\leq T_{0}$ where $T_{0}>\mathfrak{r}_{K,p,c}$},

(S298)

and
\mathfrak{K}_{K,p,c},\mathfrak{r}_{K,p,c}
are absolute positive constants. Note that the function
r_{K,p,c}
is the same as the
r(t)
function defined in
(
22
, Lemma 12)
. Furthermore, since
\mathsf{supp}(H)\subseteq[-M,M]\times[\underline{L},\overline{U}]
,

\Bigg|\mathbb{E}_{Z}\Big[e^{it_{2}\left(\mu+\sigma Z/\sqrt{K}\right)}\mid\sigma,\mu\Big]\Bigg|\leq\Bigg|\mathbb{E}_{Z}\Big[e^{it_{2}\left(\sigma Z/\sqrt{K}\right)}\mid\sigma,\mu\Big]\Bigg|\leq\exp\{-\underline{L}t^{2}_{2}/(2K)\},

almost surely under
H
.
Combining the forgoing displays with Assumption
19
and Lemma S.8 of
22
gives a constant
\mathfrak{L}(K,p,c)>0
such that

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p+1}(t_{1},t_{2};H)\big|\lesssim_{K,p,c,M,\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}|t_{1}|^{\,c+(K-p)/2-1}\exp(-\pi|t_{1}|/2)\exp(-\underline{L}^{2}t^{2}_{2}/2K),

(S299)

for all
|t_{1}|>\mathfrak{L}(K,p,c)
.

Observe that for any two priors
H_{1}
and
H_{2}
satisfying the assumptions of the lemma, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}x^{2c-1}\Big(f_{H_{1},K-p+1}(x,y)-f_{H_{2},K-p+1}(x,y)\Big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\Big|\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p+1}(t_{1},t_{2};H_{1})-\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p+1}(t_{1},t_{2};H_{2})\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|r_{K,p,c}(t_{1})|^{2}\,\Big|\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};H_{1})-\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};H_{2})\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{1}.

(S300)

For any
T>0
, we can decompose the integral into two regions:

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}}\int_{|t_{1}|\geq T}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|r_{K,p,c}(t_{1})|^{2}\,\Big|\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};H_{1})-\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};H_{2})\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{1},

(S301)

\displaystyle\mathrm{II}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}}\int_{|t_{1}|<T}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|r_{K,p,c}(t_{1})|^{2}\,\Big|\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};H_{1})-\mathcal{M}_{c,K-p}(t_{1},t_{2};H_{2})\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{2}\,\mathrm{d}t_{1}.

(S302)

Noting that

\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp(-\underline{L}t^{2}_{2}/2)\,\mathrm{d}t_{2}\lesssim_{\underline{L}}1,

we may apply the same techniques as in the proof of Lemma 12 of
22
along with (
S298
) and (
S299
) to deduce that for any
T_{0}>\mathfrak{r}_{K,p,c}\vee\mathfrak{L}(K,p,c)\vee(2c+K-p)

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,c,M,\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}T^{2c+K-p-2}_{0}\exp(-\pi T_{0}),

(S303)

\displaystyle\mathrm{II}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,c,M,\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}T_{0}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}x^{2c-1}\Big(f_{H_{1},K-p}(x,y)-f_{H_{2},K-p}(x,y)\Big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y.

(S304)

Combining the two aforementioned relations, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}x^{2c-1}\Big(f_{H_{1},K-p+1}(x,y)-f_{H_{2},K-p+1}(x,y)\Big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim_{K,p,c,M,\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}T^{2c+K-p-2}_{0}\exp(-\pi T_{0})+T_{0}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}x^{2c-1}\Big(f_{H_{1},K-p}(x,y)-f_{H_{2},K-p}(x,y)\Big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

(S305)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim_{K,p,c,M,\underline{L},\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}\exp(-\pi T_{0})+T_{0}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}x^{2c-1}\Big(f_{H_{1},K-p}(x,y)-f_{H_{2},K-p}(x,y)\Big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y,

(S306)

where the final inequality follows from (S2) of
22
.
Finally, plugging in
c=1/2
, taking
T_{0}=\mathfrak{r}_{K,p,c}\vee\mathfrak{L}(K,p,c)\vee(2c+K-p)\vee|\log\rho_{K,p}|
in the foregoing calculations, where

\smash{\rho_{K,p}:=\left\|f_{H_{1},K-p}-f_{H_{2},K-p}\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}},}

we obtain the following by retracing the steps of the proof of Lemma 12 of
22
:

\left\|f_{H_{1},K-p+1}-f_{H_{2},K-p+1}\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}\;\leq\;\Big(1+\big|\log\left\|f_{H_{1},K-p}-f_{H_{2},K-p}\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}\big|\Big)\cdot\left\|f_{H_{1},K-p}-f_{H_{2},K-p}\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}.

Finally, since

\left\|f_{H_{1},K-p}-f_{H_{2},K-p}\right\|^{2}_{L_{2}}\lesssim\mathcal{H}^{2}(f_{H_{1},K-p},f_{H_{2},K-p}),

and the map
x\mapsto x(1+|\log x|)
is monotone increasing, the assertion of Lemma
S17
follows.

L.3.2
Proof of Lemma
S18

Define

\displaystyle\mathtt{A}_{n}:=M+\sqrt{\frac{4\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}{K}\log n},\qquad\mathtt{B}_{n}:=\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}\cdot\max\left\{1,\frac{8}{K-p}\log n\right\}.

(S307)

Recall that

D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})=\frac{D_{i}(H)}{2}+\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H})}{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\max\left\{D_{i}(H),D_{i}(\widehat{H})\right\}.

Also recall the event
\mathcal{A}
defined in (
S280
).
Using the above inequality together with the fact that

\displaystyle\frac{N_{i}(z,H^{\prime})}{D_{i}(H^{\prime})}\in[0,1]\qquad\text{for any distribution }H^{\prime},

(S308)

we conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,H)-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right|\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4\,\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}\!\left(S_{i}^{2}\geq\mathtt{B}_{n}\right)\right]+4\,\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}\!\left(|A_{i}|>\mathtt{A}_{n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,H)-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right|\mathds{1}\!\left(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},\,S_{i}^{2}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n}\right)\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right].

(S309)

By definition of
\mathtt{B}_{n}
and
\mathtt{A}_{n}
, we can conclude that

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}(S^{2}_{i}\geq\mathtt{B}_{n})\right]\leq\frac{1}{n},\quad\mbox{and}\quad\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}(|A_{i}|>\mathtt{A}_{n})\right]\leq\frac{1}{n}.

Therefore, it suffices to control

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right].

By (
S283
) and Theorem
18
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})\right|

(S310)

\displaystyle=\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(s^{2})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)s^{2}}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}

(S311)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\times(f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},a)-f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},a))\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\Bigg|,

(S312)

where the absolute constant
C_{K,p}>0
is defined in the referred theorem.
Let us take
T=16\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}\cdot\max\left\{1,(K-p)^{-1}\log n\right\}
and decompose the integral in the foregoing expression as follows:

\displaystyle\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})\right|

(S313)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{T}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(S^{2}_{i})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S^{2}_{i}}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S^{2}_{i}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}

(S314)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\times(f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})-f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i}))\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\Bigg|

(S315)

\displaystyle+\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\Bigg|\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(S^{2}_{i})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S^{2}_{i}}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S^{2}_{i}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}

(S316)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\times(f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})-f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i}))\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\Bigg|.

(S317)

Using Lemma S.1 of
22
, we have that for all
distributions
\widetilde{H}
on
\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
and all
t^{2}\geq T
,

|f_{\widetilde{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},a)|\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L}}\exp\left(-\frac{(K-p)t^{2}}{8\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}\right),\qquad\text{for all }a\in\mathbb{R}.

If,
K-p>2
, if
S_{i}^{2}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n}
, retracing the proof of Lemma S.9 of

22
yields

\displaystyle\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}^{2}\,(t^{2})^{-(K-p-1)}}{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S_{i}^{2}}\mathds{1}\!\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S_{i}^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}

(S318)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,\nu}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\min\left\{\left\{(K-p)S_{i}^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})\right\}^{K-p-2},T^{-(K-p-2)}\right\}.

(S319)

If
K-p=2
, then retracing the arguments of
22
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}^{2}\,(t^{2})^{-1}}{3t^{2}-2S_{i}^{2}}\mathds{1}\!\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{2S_{i}^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{3}\right\}\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\lesssim_{K,p,\nu}\frac{1}{S^{2}_{i}}\log\left(\frac{2\nu^{2}S^{2}_{i}}{z^{2}}+1\right)\lesssim\frac{1}{z^{2}}.

(S320)

This implies, for any
K-p\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}^{2}\,(t^{2})^{-(K-p-1)}}{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S_{i}^{2}}\mathds{1}\!\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S_{i}^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}

(S321)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{z^{2}}\min\left\{\left\{(K-p)S_{i}^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})\right\}^{K-p-2},T^{-(K-p-2)}\right\}.

(S322)

Using the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality together with the above bound and the exponential tail estimate, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\Bigg|\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(S_{i}^{2})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t^{2})^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S_{i}^{2}}}\mathds{1}\!\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S_{i}^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}

(S323)

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\times\bigl(f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})-f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})\bigr)\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\nu,\underline{z}}\left\{\left(\frac{S_{i}^{2}}{T}\right)^{(K-p)/2-1}\vee 1\right\}\times\exp\left(-\frac{(K-p)T}{8\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}\right).

(S324)

Since
S_{i}^{2}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n}
and
T\geq\frac{16\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}{K-p}\log n
, the polynomial term is bounded and the exponential term satisfies

\exp\left(-\frac{(K-p)T}{8\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U}}\right)\leq\exp\left(-2\log n\right)=n^{-2}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\Bigg|\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(S_{i}^{2})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t^{2})^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S_{i}^{2}}}\mathds{1}\!\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S_{i}^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}

(S325)

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\times\bigl(f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})-f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})\bigr)\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\Bigg|

(S326)

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\nu,\underline{z}}\frac{1}{n^{2}},\qquad\text{for all }S_{i}^{2}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n}.

(S327)

This implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\times\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\Bigg|\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(S^{2}_{i})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S^{2}_{i}}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S^{2}_{i}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 100.00015pt\times(f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})-f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i}))\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\Bigg|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\nu,\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n^{2}}.

(S328)

Next, we consider the subclass of densities
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\mathcal{A}}\subset\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}
where

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\mathcal{A}}:=\left\{(f_{H^{\prime},K-p},f_{H^{\prime},K-p+1})\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt}}:\mathcal{H}^{2}\left(f_{H^{\prime},K-p},f_{H,K-p}\right)\leq C\frac{(\log n)^{3}}{n}\right\},

(S329)

for
C>0
defined Theorem
S16
. Take
\mathfrak{R}=(0,\mathtt{B}_{n}]\times[-\mathtt{A}_{n},\mathtt{A}_{n}]
and consider a
\eta=1/n
cover of the class of densities in
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\mathcal{A}}
under the semi-norm
d_{\mathfrak{R}}
(defined in (
S264
)). Let this cover be given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}=\{(f_{H_{1},K-p},f_{H_{1},K-p+1}),\ldots,(f_{H_{N},K-p},f_{H_{N},K-p+1})\}.

(S330)

Then, if
\mathcal{A}
holds, there exists
j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{(t^{2},a)\in\mathfrak{R}}\left|f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},a)-f_{H_{j},K-p+1}(t^{2},a)\right|\leq\frac{1}{n},\quad\mbox{and}

(S331)

\displaystyle\sup_{(t^{2},a)\in\mathfrak{R}}\left|f_{\widehat{H},K-p}(t^{2},a)-f_{H_{j},K-p}(t^{2},a)\right|\leq\frac{1}{n}.

(S332)

Define
\widehat{H}_{j}=(H_{j}+H)/2
and

\displaystyle N_{i}(z,G;T)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(S^{2}_{i})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S^{2}_{i}}}

(S333)

\displaystyle\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \times\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S^{2}_{i}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}f_{G,K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})\,\mathrm{d}t^{2},

for any mixing measure
G
.

Using (
L.3.2
), we can show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\times\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\Bigg|\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(S^{2}_{i})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S^{2}_{i}}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S^{2}_{i}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 100.00015pt\times(f_{H_{j},K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})-f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i}))\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\Bigg|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\nu,\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n^{2}},

(S335)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\times\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\Bigg|\int_{T}^{\infty}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(S^{2}_{i})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)S^{2}_{i}}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)S^{2}_{i}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 100.00015pt\times(f_{H_{j},K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i})-f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},A_{i}))\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\Bigg|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\nu,\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n^{2}}.

(S336)

Using the foregoing inequalities, we can decompose the left-hand side of (
S292
) as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S337)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\left|\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S338)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,H_{j})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S339)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,H_{j})-N_{i}(z,H)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S340)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,H)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\left|\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S341)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\left|\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S342)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,H)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\left|\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S343)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H}_{j};T)-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H};T)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S344)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H}_{j};T)-N_{i}(z,H;T)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\right\}\right]+\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n^{2}}

(S345)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\left|\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S346)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,H)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\left|\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S347)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H}_{j};T)-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H};T)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S348)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{k\in[N]}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H}_{k};T)-N_{i}(z,H;T)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{k})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\right\}\right)\right]+\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n^{2}}.

(S349)

If
|a|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\frac{C_{K,p}(s^{2})^{(K-p-2)/2}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)s^{2}}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}

(S350)

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\times\left|f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(a,t^{2})-f_{H_{j},K-p+1}(a,t^{2})\right|\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}

(S351)

\displaystyle\leq C_{K,p}(s^{2})^{(K-p-2)/2}\,\left(\int_{0}^{T}\frac{(t^{2})^{-(K-p-1)}}{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)s^{2}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\right)^{1/2}\times

(S352)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt\left(\int_{0}^{T}(f_{\widehat{H},K-p+1}(a,t^{2})-f_{H_{j},K-p+1}(a,t^{2}))^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\right)^{1/2}

(S353)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\nu,\underline{z}}\frac{C_{K,p}(s^{2})^{(K-p-2)/2}}{\sqrt{z}((K-p+1)s^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2}))^{\frac{K-p-2}{2}}}\times\frac{(\log n)^{1/2}}{n},

(S354)

where the last inequality follows from the Cauchy Schwartz inequality, (
S331
), and the techniques outlined in the proof of Lemma S9 of
22
. Therefore, for all
|z|\geq\underline{z}
we have

\displaystyle\left|N_{i}(z,{H}_{j};T)-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H};T)\right|\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\nu,\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{1/2}}{n},\quad\mbox{if $|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n}$ and $\mathcal{A}$ holds.}

Observe that
\smash{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})\geq\max\left\{\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H})}{2},\frac{D_{i}(H)}{2}\right\}}
implies

\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\leq 2,\quad\mbox{and}\quad\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,H)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\leq 2,\quad\mbox{for all $i=1,\ldots,n$.}

Using the foregoing inequalities and (
S331
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left(\frac{N_{i}(z,\mathrm{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right)\left|\frac{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})-D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S356)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{n},\quad\mbox{where $\mathrm{H}\in\{\widehat{H},H\}$.}

(S357)

Furthermore, using (
L.3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H}_{j};T)-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H};T)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n}.

Combining (
S356
) and (
L.3.2
) with (
S337
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H})-N_{i}(z,\widehat{H})}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\cdot\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S359)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n}+\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{k=1,\ldots,N}V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)\right],

(S360)

where for any
k\in\{1,\ldots,N\}

V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H}_{k};T)-N_{i}(z,H;T)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{k})}\right|\penalty\ \mathds{1}(|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},S^{2}_{i}\leq\mathtt{B}_{n})\right\}.

Therefore, it suffices to control

\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{k=1,\ldots,N}V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)\right].

Next, observe that since

\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\frac{N_{i}(z,{H}_{j};T)-N_{i}(z,H;T)}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{j})}\right|\leq 2,\quad\mbox{for all $i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}$ and $j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$.}

Therefore, for two sequences of points

\mathfrak{a}=\left\{(s^{2}_{1},a_{1}),\ldots,(s^{2}_{i},a_{i}),\ldots,(s^{2}_{n},a_{n})\right\},\quad\mbox{and}\quad\mathfrak{a}^{\prime}=\left\{(s^{2}_{1},a_{1}),\ldots,((s^{\prime}_{i})^{2},a^{\prime}_{i}),\ldots,(s^{2}_{n},a_{n})\right\}

differeing only at the
i
-th entry we have

\displaystyle|V_{k}\left(\mathfrak{a}\right)-V_{k}\left(\mathfrak{a}^{\prime}\right)|\leq\frac{2}{n}.

(S361)

Using the bounded differences inequality
(
4
, Theorem 6.2)
, we get for all
j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}

\mathbb{P}\Big[\left|V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)\right]\right|>t\Big]\leq\exp\left(-\frac{nt^{2}}{4}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\max_{k\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\left|V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)\right]\right|\Bigg]

(S362)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{k\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\left|V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{k}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)\right]\right|>t\right]\,\mathrm{d}t

(S363)

\displaystyle=x_{0}+N\int_{x_{0}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{nt^{2}}{4}\right)\,\mathrm{d}t\leq x_{0}+\frac{2N}{nx_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{nx^{2}_{0}}{4}\right),\quad\mbox{for any $x_{0}>0$.}

(S364)

Taking
x_{0}\asymp_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\sqrt{\frac{8\log N}{n}}
. Using Lemma
S15
and some algebraic manipulation, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\max_{j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\left|V_{j}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[V_{j}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)\right]\right|\Bigg]\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}.

(S365)

Finally, we must control

\max_{j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\left[V_{j}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)\right].

In that direction, proceeding as in the proof of Lemma S9 of
22
and using the definition of
V_{1},\ldots,V_{N}
, we can get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[V_{j}\left((S^{2}_{1},A_{1}),\ldots,(S^{2}_{n},A_{n})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim 2\int_{0}^{\mathtt{B}_{n}}\int_{-\mathtt{A}_{n}}^{\mathtt{A}_{n}}\sup_{|z|\geq\underline{z}}\left|\int_{0}^{T}\frac{C_{K,p}\,(s^{2})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)s^{2}}}\times\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt\left.\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+(z^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}(f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},a)-f_{H_{j},K-p+1}(t^{2},a))\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\right|\mathrm{d}a\,\mathrm{d}s^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2\int_{0}^{\mathtt{B}_{n}}\int_{-\mathtt{A}_{n}}^{\mathtt{A}_{n}}\int_{0}^{T}\left|\frac{C_{K,p}\,(s^{2})^{(K-p)/2-1}(t)^{-(K-p-1)}}{\sqrt{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)s^{2}}}\times\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt\left.\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+(\underline{z}^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}(f_{H,K-p+1}(t^{2},a)-f_{H_{j},K-p+1}(t^{2},a))\right|\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\,\mathrm{d}a\,\mathrm{d}s^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p}\int_{0}^{\mathtt{B}_{n}}\Bigg\{\left(\int_{-\mathtt{A}_{n}}^{\mathtt{A}_{n}}\int_{0}^{T}\frac{(t^{2})^{-(K-p-1)}}{(K-p+1)t^{2}-(K-p)s^{2}}\mathds{1}\left\{t^{2}\geq\frac{(K-p)s^{2}+(\underline{z}^{2}/\nu^{2})}{K-p+1}\right\}\,\mathrm{d}t^{2}\,\mathrm{d}a\right)\Bigg\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\times(s^{2})^{(K-p)/2-1}\left\|f_{{H}_{j},K-p+1}-f_{H,K-p+1}\right\|_{L_{2}}\,\mathrm{d}s^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\mathtt{B}_{n}\sqrt{\mathtt{A}_{n}}\times\left\|f_{{H}_{j},K-p+1}-f_{H,K-p+1}\right\|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\mathtt{B}_{n}\sqrt{\mathtt{A}_{n}}\times\mathcal{H}(f_{{H}_{j},K-p},f_{H,K-p})\left|\log\mathcal{H}^{2}(f_{{H}_{j},K-p},f_{H,K-p})\right|^{1/2}.

(S366)

In the foregoing display, the penultimate inequality follows using the arguments similar to (
S350
).
Using Lemma
S17
and the definition of the class of densities
\mathcal{A}
, we can conclude from the above display that

\mathbb{E}\left[V_{j}\left((A_{1},S^{2}_{1}),\ldots,(A_{n},S^{2}_{n})\right)\right]\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{13/4}}{\sqrt{n}},\quad\mbox{for all $j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$.}

Therefore,

\max_{j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\left[V_{j}\left((A_{1},S^{2}_{1}),\ldots,(A_{n},S^{2}_{n})\right)\right]\lesssim_{K,p,M,\macc@depth\char 1\relax\macc@set@skewchar\macc@nested@a 111{U},\underline{L},\underline{z}}\frac{(\log n)^{13/4}}{\sqrt{n}}.

Combining the above display with (
S359
) and (
S365
), the result follows.

L.4
Proof of Lemma
S19

To show this theorem, recall
\mathcal{S}
, the
\eta=1/n
cover of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{jt},\mathcal{A}}
defined in (
S264
) and
\mathfrak{R}=(0,\mathtt{B}_{n}]\times[-\mathtt{A}_{n},\mathtt{A}_{n}]
where
\mathtt{A}_{n}
and
\mathtt{B}_{n}
are defined in (
S307
). If
\mathcal{A}
holds, then we can get an index
\widehat{j}\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
such that

\sup_{(t^{2},a)\in\mathfrak{R}}\left|f_{\widehat{H},K-p}(t^{2},a)-f_{H_{\widehat{j}},K-p}(t^{2},a)\right|\leq\frac{1}{n}.

Let us further define the event

\mathcal{E}:=\left\{|A_{i}|\leq\mathtt{A}_{n},\,S_{i}^{2}\in(0,\mathtt{B}_{n}],\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ \text{for all $i=1,\ldots,n$}\right\}.

By the construction of
\mathtt{A}_{n}
and
\mathtt{B}_{n}
in (
S307
), one can get that
\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}^{c}]\leq n^{-1}
.
Now consider the decomposition

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\frac{|D_{i}(H)-D_{i}(\widehat{H})|}{D_{i}(\widehat{H}_{\star})}\,\mathds{1}(\mathcal{A})\right]

(S367)
